# Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot

ברטנורא על משנה יבמות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Nashim

## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:1)


**חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה נָשִׁים פּוֹטְרוֹת צָרוֹתֵיהֶן.** שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה אַחַת מֵהֶן נְשׂוּאָה לְאָחִיו וְלוֹ שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים, שְׁתֵּיהֶן פְּטוּרוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ויקרא יח) וְאִשָּׁה אֶל אֲחוֹתָהּ לֹא תִקַּח לִצְרֹר לְגַלּוֹת עֶרְוָתָהּ עָלֶיהָ, שֶׁאֵין תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר עָלֶיהָ, אֶלָּא לְלַמֶּדְךָ שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יְבִמְתּוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהּ (דברים כה) יְבָמָהּ יָבֹא עָלֶיהָ, אִם הִיא אִשָּׁה אֶל אֲחוֹתָהּ, לֹא תִקַּח. וְהוּא הַדִּין לִשְׁאָר עֲרָיוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן כָּרֵת. וְאֵין לִי אֶלָּא הִיא, צָרָתָהּ מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר לִצוֹר. צָרַת צָרָתָהּ מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר לִצְרֹר. דְּמַשְׁמַע לֹא תִקַּח לֹא הִיא וְלֹא צָרָתָהּ וְלֹא צָרַת צָרָתָהּ:


חמש עשרה נשים פוטרות צרותיהן – for one of them was married to his brother, and he had two wives and died without children, both of them are exempt [Halitzah and levirate marriage] , as it states (Leviticus 18:18): “Do not marry a woman as a rival to her sister and uncover her nakedness in the other’s [lifetime],” for the inference does not teach us about her but rather to teach you that even his sister-in-law (i.e., the widow of a brother who died without issue) that is stated about her (Deuteronomy 25:5): “Her husband’s brother shall unite with her: [he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty].” If she is “do not marry a woman as a rival to her sister” and the same law applies to the rest of the forbidden relationships [in the Torah] that have [the punishment of] extirpation. But I don’t have anything but her, her rival-wife, from where do we learn this in the Torah? The inference teaches us לצור צרתה (I Samuel 1:6) – “her rival”; from where do we learn in the Torah צרתה? The inference teaches us (Leviticus 18:18): לצרור – “as a rival,” which implies “do not marry,” neither he nor her rival wife, nor the rival of her rival wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:2)


**בִּתּוֹ וּבַת בִּתּוֹ וְכוּ'.** בְּבִתּוֹ מֵאֲנוּסָתוֹ, וְכֵן בַּת בִּתּוֹ וּבַת בְּנוֹ, דְּאִלּוּ בִּתּוֹ מֵאִשְׁתּוֹ הַיְנוּ בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ. דְּכֵיוָן דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יח) עֶרְוַת אִשָּׁה וּבִתָּהּ, לֹא שְׁנָא הֵימֶנּוּ וְלֹא שְׁנָא מֵאִישׁ אַחֵר. אֲבָל בִּתּוֹ מֵאֲנוּסָתוֹ לֹא נָפֵיק מֵהַאי קְרָא, דְּאִשָּׁה עַל יְדֵי קִדּוּשִׁין מַשְׁמַע, אֶלָּא נָפְקָא מֵעֶרְוַת בַּת בִּנְךָ אוֹ בַּת בִּתְּךָ (שם):


בתו ובת בתו וכו' – regarding his daughter from his outraged wife, and similarly, the daughter of his daughter and the daughter of his son, for whereas his daughter from his wife which is the daughter of his wife for since it is written (Leviticus 18:17): [“Do not uncover the nakedness of] a woman and her daughter,” there is no difference whether it is from him neither there is any difference if it is from another man, but his daughter from his outraged wife, one does not derive from this Biblical verse, because it implies a wife through betrothal/Kiddushin, but it is derived from (Leviticus 18:17): “[nor shall you marry] her son’s daughter or her daughter’s daughter [and uncover her nakedness: they are kindred; it is depravity].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:3)


**חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חָמִיו.** כָּל אֵלּוּ אֲסוּרוֹת לוֹ מִשּׁוּם עֶרְוַת אִשָּׁה וּבִתָּהּ לֹא תְגַלֶּה אֶת בַּת בְּנָהּ וְאֶת בַּת בִּתָּהּ, וְיֵשׁ בִּכְלַל זֶה חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חָמִיו:


חמותו ואם חמותו ואם חמיו – all of these are forbidden to him because of (Leviticus 18:17): “Do not uncover the nakedness of a woman and her daughter; nor shall you marry her son’s daughter or her daughter’s daughter [and uncover her nakedness];” there is included within this his mother-in-law, the mother of his mother-in-law and the mother of his father-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:4)


**אֲחוֹתוֹ מֵאִמּוֹ.** שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְאָחִיו מֵאָבִיו וּמֵת. וְכֵן אֲחוֹת אִמּוֹ. שֶׁאֵין יִבּוּם אֶלָּא בְּאָחִיו מִן הָאָב, דְּיָלְפִינַן אַחֲוָה אַחֲוָה מִבְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב, מַה לְּהַלָּן מִן הָאָב אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מִן הָאֵם, הָכָא נַמִּי לֹא שְׁנָא:


אחותו מאמו – [his sister from his mother] who was married to his brother from his father, and he died, and similarly, the sister of his mother, for there is no levirate marriage, other than with his brother from his father for we derive “his brother, his brother” from the children of Jacob; just as there it is from the father, even though that there are none from the mother, so too here, there is no difference.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:5)


**וְאֵשֶׁת אָחִיו מֵאִמּוֹ.** שֶׁמֵּת וּנְשָׂאָהּ אָחִיו מֵאָבִיו, שֶׁהָיְתָה נָכְרִית אֶצְלוֹ, וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים וְנָפְלָה לְפָנָיו לְיִבּוּם. וַאֲסוּרָה לוֹ לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה תְּחִלָּה אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו מֵאִמּוֹ וַאֲסוּרָה לוֹ עוֹלָמִית, דִּכְתִיב (שם) עֶרְוַת אֵשֶׁת אָחִיךָ, וְדָרְשִׁינַן אָחִיךָ בֵּין מִן הָאָב בֵּין מִן הָאֵם:


ואשת אחיו מאמו – [the wife of his brother from his mother] who died and she married his [the dead husband’s] brother from his father for she was a stranger regarding him, and he died without children and she fell before him for levirate marriage but she is forbidden to him because she was first the wife of his brother from his mother, and is prohibited to him forever, as it is written (Leviticus 18:16): “Do not uncover the nakedness of your brother’s wife; [it is the nakedness of your brother],” and we expound, “your brother,” whether from the father or whether from the mother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:6)


**וְאֵשֶׁת אָחִיו שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְעוֹלָמוֹ.** כְּגוֹן רְאוּבֵן שֶׁמֵּת בְּלֹא בָנִים, וְנוֹלַד לוֹ אָח לְאַחַר מִיתָה וּשְׁמוֹ לֵוִי, וְיִבֵּם שִׁמְעוֹן אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וְלוֹ אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת, וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים, וְנָפְלוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן לִפְנֵי לֵוִי, שְׁתֵּיהֶן פְּטוּרוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁאֵשֶׁת רְאוּבֵן שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְשִׁמְעוֹן אֲסוּרָה לְלֵוִי בְּכָרֵת, לְפִי שֶׁמִּעֲטָהּ הַכָּתוּב מִיִּבּוּם לֵוִי, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים כה) כִּי יֵשְׁבוּ אַחִים יַחְדָּו, שֶׁהָיְתָה לָהֶם יְשִׁיבָה אַחַת בָּעוֹלָם. הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁמֵּת רְאוּבֵן, נֶאֶסְרָה עַל לֵוִי אִסּוּר עוֹלָם כְּאֵשֶׁת אָח שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בָּנִים. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעַתָּה נָפְלָה מִכֹּחַ נִשּׂוּאֵי שִׁמְעוֹן שֶׁהָיָה בְּעוֹלָמוֹ, אֲסוּרָה לוֹ מֵחֲמַת רְאוּבֵן. וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁהִיא אֲסוּרָה, כָּךְ צָרָתָהּ אֲסוּרָה:


ואשת אחיו שלא היה בעולמו – such as, for example, Reuven who died without children and a brother was born to him after his death and his name is Levi, but Shimon performed levirate marriage with his wife and he has another wife and he died without children, and both of them fell before Levi, both of them are exempt, since the wife of Reuven who married Shimon is forbidden to Levi through [the punishment of] extirpation because Scripture had exempted him from Levirate marriage from Levi, as it states (Deuteronomy 25:5): “When brothers dwell together [and one of them dies and leaves no son the wife of the deceased shall not be married to a stranger, outside of her family: Her husband’s brother shall unite with her: he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty],” that they had one seating in the world; therefore, when Reuven died , she was forbidden upon Levi forever as the brother’s wife who has children and even though that now she fell from the power of the marriage of Shimon who was prohibited to him in his world on account of Reuven, and just as she is prohibited, so too is her rival wife forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:7)


**וְכַלָּתוֹ.** שֶׁמֵּת בְּנוֹ, וּנְשָׂאָהּ אָחִיו. וְכַלָּתוֹ אֲסוּרָה עָלָיו אִסּוּר עוֹלָם וַאֲפִלּוּ אַחַר שֶׁמֵּת בְּנוֹ:


וכלתו – [his former daughter-in-law – who then married his brother] whose son died, and she married his brother and his daughter-in-law is prohibited to him eternally and even after his son died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:8)


**וְכֻלָּן אִם מֵתוּ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁמֵּתָה בִּתּוֹ קֹדֶם מִיתַת אָחִיו אוֹ שֶׁמֵּאֲנָה בּוֹ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מֵאוּן אֶלָּא בִּיתוֹמָה קְטַנָּה שֶׁהִשִּׂיאוּהָ אִמָּהּ וְאַחֶיהָ, מַשְׁכַּחַת לָהּ שֶׁמֵּאֲנָה בְּחַיֵּי אָבִיהָ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִשִּׂיאָהּ אָבִיהָ לְאַחֵר וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה בְּעוֹדָהּ קְטַנָּה, שֶׁשּׁוּב אֵין לְאָבִיהָ רְשׁוּת בָּהּ, וְנִשֵּׂאת לַאֲחִי אָבִיהָ בְּעוֹדָהּ קְטַנָּה, אָז יוֹצְאָה בְּמֵאוּן, דְּקָרִינַן לָהּ לְקַמָּן יְתוֹמָה בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב. וְאִם מֵאֲנָה בְּאָחִיו אוֹ נִתְגָּרְשָׁה מִמֶּנּוּ, אוֹ נִמְצְאָה אַיְלוֹנִית שֶׁמִּקְחָהּ מֶקַּח טָעוּת וּכְאִלּוּ לֹא הָיְתָה אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו מֵעוֹלָם, צָרָתָהּ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת:


וכולן אם מתו – such as, for example, this son died before the death of his brother ore she refused him, and even though there is no refusal other than with an minor female orphan whose mother or her mothers married her off, it is found regarding her that she refused him during the lifetime of her father, such as, for example, that her father married her off to another and she was divorced while still a minor, her father does not have control over her and she married off to the brothers of her father while she was still a minor, then she goes free through refusal and we call her further on an orphan during the lifetime of [her] father and if she refused his brother or was divorced from him or was found to be a barren woman whose acquisition was an errant purchase, it is as if she was never the wife of his brother, her rival/co-wife engages in a levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:9)


**אַיְלוֹנִית.** לְשׁוֹן אַיִל, הַזָּכָר מִן הַצֹּאן. וְסִימָנֶיהָ מְפֹרָשִׁים, שֶׁאֵין לָהּ שָׁדַיִם כְּנָשִׁים, וְאֵין לָהּ סִימָנִים, וְקוֹלָהּ עָבֶה כְּאִישׁ, וְאֵין לָהּ שִׁפּוּלֵי מֵעַיִם כְּנָשִׁים, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם אֵינוֹ בּוֹלֵט מִגּוּפָהּ כְּעֶרְוַת שְׁאָר הַנָּשִׁים:


אילוני' – the language of a male ram from the sheep and her signs are explained clearly – that she does not have breasts like women and she does not have signs of puberty and her voice is thick like that of a male and she does not have the lower part of her abdomen like women , meaning to say, that this place does not protrude from her body like the lewdness of other women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:1:10)


**וְאִי אַתָּה יָכוֹל לוֹמַר וְכוּ'.** שֶׁכְּבָר יָלְדוּ מֵאַחֵר קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּשְּׂאוּ לְאָחִיו, הִלְכָּךְ לָאו אַיְלוֹנִיּוֹת נִינְהוּ. וּמֵאוּן נַמִּי לֵיכָּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי גְּדוֹלוֹת הֵן, וְאֵין מְמָאֶנֶת אֶלָּא קְטַנָּה. וְתַנָּא דִּידַן דְּלֹא חָשִׁיב אִמּוֹ בִּכְלָל הַנָּשִׁים שֶׁפּוֹטְרוֹת צָרוֹתֵיהֶן, סָבַר לָהּ כְּמַאן דְּאָמַר שֶׁאֵין אָדָם נוֹשֵׂא אֲנוּסַת אָבִיו וּמְפֻתַּת אָבִיו. אֲבָל לְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר נוֹשֵׂא אָדָם אֲנוּסַת אָבִיו וּמְפֻתַּת אָבִיו, וְהִיא הֲלָכָה, אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁתִּנָּשֵׂא אִמּוֹ לְאָחִיו מֵאָבִיו, וּכְשֶׁיָּמוּת בְּלֹא בָנִים נִמְצֵאת אִמּוֹ נוֹפֶלֶת לִפְנֵי בְּנָהּ לְיִבּוּם, וְנִמְצְאוּ שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה נָשִׁים פּוֹטְרוֹת צָרוֹתֵיהֶן, וְאִמּוֹ אַחַת מֵהֶן. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


ואי אתה יכול לומר וכו' – that they already give birth from another prior to her being married to his brother; therefore, these are not barren women and there is also no refusal, for they are adults and only a minor [woman] may refuse, but our Tanna/teacher does not consider his mother amidst the women who exempt their rival/co-wives, for he holds like the one who holds that a man does not marry the outranged woman of his father and seduced woman of his father, but according to the one who says , that a man may marry the outraged wife of his father and the seduced wife of his and this is Halakha. It is possible that his mother married his brother from his father, and when he dies without children, it is found that his mother falls before her son for levirate marriage, and there are found sixteen women who exempt their rival/co-wives, and is mother is one of them, and this is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:1)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁבִּתּוֹ פְּטוּרָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי אֲסוּרָה לוֹ, וְאֵין שָׁם אָח אֶלָּא הוּא, כָּךְ צָרָתָהּ פְּטוּרָה:


כשם שבתו פטורה – for [his daughter] is prohibited to him and there is no brother other than him, hence her rival/co-wife is also exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:2)


**הָלְכָה צָרַת בִּתּוֹ וְנִשֵּׂאת לְאָחִיו הַשֵּׁנִי.** הֵיכָא דְּיֵשׁ אָח אַחֵר שֶׁשְּׁתֵּיהֶן מֻתָּרוֹת לוֹ, וְאֵינָן פְּטוּרוֹת, שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ כָּאן מִצְוַת יִבּוּם, וּמִתְיַבֶּמֶת הָאַחַת וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה פְּטוּרָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) אֶת בֵּית אָחִיו, בַּיִת אֶחָד הוּא בּוֹנֶה וְאֵין בּוֹנֶה שְׁתֵּי בָּתִּים. וְאִם יִבֵּם אָחִיו הַשֵּׁנִי אֶת צָרַת בִּתּוֹ וְלוֹ אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים וְחָזְרוּ וְנָפְלוּ לְפָנָיו:


הלכה צרת בתו ונשאת לקאחיו השני – where there is another brother that both of them are permitted to hi, and are not exempt, for there is here the commandment of levirate marriage and one of them engages in levirate marriage and the other is exempt, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:9): “[Thus shall be done to the man who will not build up] his brother’s house;” he builds one house, but he does not build two homes, and if the second brother performed a levirate marriage with the rival wife of his daughter, and he [himself] has another wife, and he dies without children, and they returned and fell before him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:3)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁצָּרַת בִּתּוֹ פְּטוּרָה.** שֶׁנֶּאֶסְרָה עָלָיו מִשְּׁעַת נְפִילַת אָחִיו הָרִאשׁוֹן, כָּךְ צָרַת צָרָתָהּ, אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו הַשֵּׁנִי הָאַחֶרֶת, פְּטוּרָה, שֶׁזּוֹ פּוֹטַרְתָּהּ, שֶׁצָּרַת עֶרְוָה פּוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ:


כשם שצרת בתו פטורה – that she became forbidden upon him from the time of the falling of his first brother, so too is the latter’s present associate/rival wife of his second brother’s wife is exempt, for this woman exempts her for the rivals of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity exempts her rival/co-wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:4)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵם מֵאָה.** אַחִים, וְהָלְכָה צָרַת צָרָתָהּ שֶׁל בִּתּוֹ וְנִתְיַבְּמָה לְאָחִיו הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְלוֹ אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָּנִים, שְׁתֵּיהֶן אֲסוּרוֹת עַל זֶה. וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם:


ואפילו הם מאה – [and even if they are one-hundred] brothers, and the rivals of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity of his daughter went and engaged in levirate marriage with his third brother, and he has another wife, and he died without children, both of them are forbidden to each other, and similarly forever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:5)


**צָרָתָהּ מֻתֶּרֶת.** לְהִתְיַבֵּם. שֶׁהֲרֵי בִּשְׁעַת זִקַּת יִבּוּם אֵינָהּ צָרָתָהּ:


צרתה מותרת – to engage in levirate marriage for at the time of the levirate relation, she was not her rival wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:2:6)


**וְכָל הַיְּכוֹלָה לְמָאֵן.** שֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה הָעֶרְוָה, וִיכוֹלָה לְמָאֵן וְלֹא מֵאֲנָה, וּמֵת אָחִיו, הוֹאִיל וְקִדּוּשֶׁיהָ אֵינָן אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן וְזִקָּה שֶׁלָּהּ אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, אֵינָהּ פּוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ מִן הַחֲלִיצָה. וּלְהִתְיַבֵּם אֲסוּרָה, שֶׁנִּרְאֵית כְּצָרַת עֶרְוָה:


וכל היכולה למאן – when she is a minor, [the woman forbidden to a man] and she is able to refuse, but she did not refuse, and his brother died, for since [her betrothal/Kiddushin] is not other than Rabbinic and her levirate relation is not other than Rabbinic, she does not exempt her rival/co-wife from Halitzah but to engage in levirate marriage is forbidden for she appears as forbidden on account of consanguinity.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:1)


**שֵׁשׁ עֲרָיוֹת חֲמוּרוֹת מֵאֵלּוּ.** וּמַה הִיא חֻמְרָא שֶׁלָּהֶן, שֶׁנְּשׂוּאוֹת לַאֲחֵרִים, וְאֵינָן יְכוֹלוֹת לְהִנָּשֵׂא לְאָחִיו שֶׁל זֶה מֵאָבִיו, וְאִם מֵתוּ בַּעֲלֵיהֶן שֶׁהֵן נָכְרִים אֵצֶל זֶה, צָרוֹתֵיהֶן מֻתָּרוֹת לִנָּשֵׂא לָזֶה, שֶׁאֵין צָרַת עֶרְוָה אֲסוּרָה אֶלָּא בְּנוֹפֶלֶת לְפָנָיו לְיִבּוּם מֵאָחִיו. וְרַבֵּנוּ מֹשֶׁה בַּר מַיְמוֹן פֵּרֵשׁ, אִם נִשְּׂאוּ בַּעֲבֵרָה לְאָחִיו, וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים וְנָפְלוּ לְיִבּוּם לְפָנָיו, צָרוֹתֵיהֶן מֻתָּרוֹת, שֶׁאֵין אֵלּוּ צָרוֹת עֶרְוָה, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיוּ קִדּוּשִׁין תּוֹפְסִין לְאָחִיו בְּאוֹתָן עֲרָיוֹת:


שש עריות חמורות מאלו – and what is their stringency? That they are married to others (i.e., outsiders). But they are not able to marry the brother of this one from his father, and if their husbands died, who are “strangers” (i.e., outsiders) regarding this one, their rival/co-wives are permitted to marry to this one, for the rivals of a woman are not forbidden on account of consanguinity other than when she falls before him for levirate marriage from his brother. And our Rabbi/teacher, Moshe bar Maimon (i.e., Maimonides) explained, that if they married through sin to his brother and he died without children, and they fell [before him] for levirate marriage, their rival/co-wives are permitted, for these are not [forbidden] on account of consanguinity since their betrothal/Kiddushin do not take effect to his brother in those forbidden relations of incest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:2)


**אִמּוֹ.** אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לְהִנָּשֵׂא לְאָחִיו מֵאָבִיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא לוֹ אֵשֶׁת הָאָב, לְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר שֶׁאֲנוּסַת אָבִיו אֲסוּרָה. וּלְעֵיל פֵּרַשְׁתִּי שֶׁאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא אִמּוֹ פּוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ, שֶׁהִיא יְכוֹלָה לְהִנָּשֵׂא לְאָחִיו מֵאָבִיו:


אמו – she cannot marry his brother from his father, for she is to him the wife of the father, according to the one who says that the ravaged wife of his father is prohibited, and above (Mishnah two), I explained, that this is not the Halakha, but rather, his mother exempts her rival/co-wife, for she is able to marry to his brother from his father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:3:3)


**אֵשֶׁת אָבִיו, אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו מֵאָבִיו.** [שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ בָּנִים] וַאֲחוֹת אָבִיו, כֻּלָּן אֲסוּרוֹת לְאָחִיו כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֲסוּרוֹת עָלָיו, וְאֵין בָּהֶם צַד יִבּוּם עַל זֶה לְעוֹלָם. וְאִם נְשָׂאוּם אֲחֵרִים וְלָהֶם נָשִׁים אֲחֵרוֹת וּמֵתוּ, צָרוֹתֵיהֶן מֻתָּרוֹת לִנָּשֵׂא לָזֶה. וְכֵן אִם נָשָׂא אוֹתָהּ אָחִיו בַּעֲבֵרָה וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים, צָרוֹתֵיהֶן מֻתָּרוֹת:


אשת אביו אשת אחיו מאביו – [that he had children] and the sister of his father, all of them are forbidden to his brother, like they are forbidden to him, and there is not side of Levirate marriage on this forever, and if others married them and they have other wives, and they (i.e., the husbands) died, their rival/co-wives are permitted to marry this one, and similarly, if his brother married them in sin, and he died without children, their rival/co-wives are permitted.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:1)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַתִּירִין.** צָרַת עֶרְוָה לְהִתְיַבֵּם לְאָחִיו. דְּלֵית לְהוּ הַךְ דְּרָשָׁה דְּלִצְרֹר:


בית שמאי מתירין – the rival of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity is to be married by the levir, for they (i.e., the School of Shammai) lack this exposition/Midrashic interpretation of (Leviticus 18:18): “[Do not marry a woman] as a rival [to her sister and uncover her nakedness in the other’s lifetime].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:2)


**חָלְצוּ.** הַצָּרוֹת מִן הָאַחִין:


חלצו – [they performed the ritual of Halitzah] the rival/co-wives from the brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי פּוֹסְלִין.** הַצָּרוֹת מִן הַכְּהֻנָּה, שֶׁחֲלִיצָתָן חֲלִיצָה:


בית שמאי פוסלין – the rival/co-wives from the priesthood, since their [act of] Halitzah is [considered] Halitzah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מַכְשִׁירִין.** שֶׁחֲלִיצָתָן שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ הָיְתָה, וַהֲרֵי הִיא כְּחוֹלֶצֶת מִן נָכְרִי:


ובית הלל מכשירין – that their [act of] Halitzah was not a necessity and she is considered as one who performed Halitzah from a heathen/foreigner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:5)


**נִתְיַבְּמוּ.** לָאַחִין:


נתיבמו – to brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:6)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַכְשִׁירִין.** אוֹתָן לַכֹּהֲנִים אִם נִתְאַלְמְנוּ מִיְבָמֵיהֶם:


בית שמאי מכשירין – [The School of Shammai validates them] to Kohanim, if they had become widowed from their levirs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל פּוֹסְלִים.** שֶׁנִּבְעֲלוּ לָאָסוּר לָהֶם, וְהַנִּבְעֶלֶת לָאָסוּר לָהּ עֲשָׂאָהּ זוֹנָה, וְזוֹנָה אֲסוּרָה לְכֹהֵן:


ובית הלל פוסלים – that they had engaged in sexual relationship with those forbidden to them, and one who engages in sexual relations with one prohibited to her makes her a harlot, and a harlot is prohibited to [marry] a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:8)


**לֹא נִמְנְעוּ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּנֵי הַצָּרוֹת שֶׁנִּתְיַבְּמוּ כְּדִבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַמְזֵרִים הֵם לְבֵית הַלֵּל, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּאִסּוּר אֵשֶׁת אָח הֵם עֲלֵיהֶם וְאֵשֶׁת אָח בְּכָרֵת, וּבְנֵי חַיָּבֵי כְּרִיתוּת מַמְזֵרִים הֵם, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן לֹא נִמְנְעוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל מִלִּשָּׂא נָשִׁים מִבֵּית שַׁמַּאי, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹדִיעִים לָהֶם אוֹתָן הַבָּאוֹת מִן הַצָּרוֹת וּפוֹרְשִׁין מֵהֶן:


לא נמנעו – and even though that the children of the rival/co-wives who had been married by the levir that according to the School of Shammai are Mamzerim/illegitimate according to the School of Hillel, and they are [included] in the prohibition of marrying the wife of one’s brother upon them, and the [violation of] the [marriage of the] wife of one’s brother is [liable to] extirpation, and those who are liable for extirpation are considered Mamzerim/illegitimate, nevertheless, [members of] the School of Hillel were not prevented from marrying women from the School of Shammai, because they would inform them of those women coming from the rival/co-wives and separate from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 1:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/1:4:9)


**אֵלּוּ עַל גַּבֵּי אֵלּוּ.** מַשְׁאִילִים כְּלֵיהֶם אֵלּוּ לְאֵלּוּ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ אֲהָא דִּתְנַן בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מַתִּירִין אֶת הַצָּרוֹת לָאַחִים וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹסְרִים, קָרֵי כָּאן לֹא תִתְגּוֹדְדוּ, לֹא תַעֲשׂוּ אֲגוּדוֹת אֲגוּדוֹת. וּמְשָׁנֵי, כְּגוֹן בֵּית דִּין אֶחָד בְּעִיר אַחַת, פְּלַג מוֹרִים כְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי וּפְלַג מוֹרִים כְּבֵית הִלֵּל. אֲבָל שְׁנֵי בָּתֵּי דִּינִים בְּעִיר אַחַת, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שְׁנֵי בָּתֵּי דִּינִים בִּשְׁתֵּי עֲיָרוֹת, לֵית לָן בָּהּ:


אלו על גבי אלו – they would loan their utensils one to the other. And in the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 14a), raises an objection on that which is taught [in our Mishnah]: “The School of Shammai declares that the rival/co-wives are permitted [to enter into levirate marriage with] the brothers and the School of Hillel [prohibits] . They call it here (Deuteronomy 14:1): “You shall not gash yourselves/לא תתגדדו “ – do not form yourselves into religious [factions]. And they responded – such as the case of one Jewish court in one city – where half teach according to the School of Shammai and half teach according to the School of Hillel, but [where there are] two Jewish courts in one city, and all the more so, two Jewish courts in two towns, it does not matter.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:1)


**כֵּיצַד אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו וְכוּ'.** פּוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ:


כיצד אשת אחיו שלא היה בעולמו – she exempts her rival/co-wife [from the requirement of levirate marriage or Halitzah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:2)


**וְנוֹלַד לָהֶן אָח.** וּמְצָאָהּ זְקוּקָה לְיָבָם, וְעָלָיו אֲסוּרָה מִשּׁוּם אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּעוֹלָמוֹ:


ונולד להן אח – and was found to be dependent upon a levir, but upon him she is prohibited because of [the prohibition of] the brother’s wife, for he was not a contemporary [having not yet been born].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:3)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִבֵּם הַשֵּׁנִי אֶת אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו.** וְלוֹ אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת:


ואח"כ יבם השני את אשת אחיו – and he has another wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:4)


**וָמֵת.** בְּלֹא בָנִים:


ומת – without children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:5)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה.** הִיא אֵשֶׁת הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁנָּפְלָה לְפָנָיו כְּבָר פַּעַם אַחַת, יוֹצֵאת מִשּׁוּם אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּעוֹלָמוֹ:


הראשונה – she is the wife of the first brother who fell before him already one time, goes forth because of [the prohibition of] being the wife of his brother who had not yet been born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:6)


**עָשָׂה בָהּ.** זֶה שֶׁמֵּת:


עשה בה – this one who died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:7)


**מַאֲמָר.** פֵּרוּשׁ שֶׁקִּדְּשָׁהּ בְּכֶסֶף. וּבִיבָמָה קִדּוּשִׁין שֶׁל כֶּסֶף אֵינָם קִדּוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים אֶלָּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, שֶׁאֵין הַיְּבָמָה נִקְנֵית לַיָּבָם לִהְיוֹת כְּאֵשֶׁת אִישׁ גְּמוּרָה עַד שֶׁיָּבֹא עָלֶיהָ, וְזֶה לֹא הִסְפִּיק לְכָנְסָהּ עַד שֶׁמֵּת:


מאמר – the explanation he betrothed her with money (as opposed to a document or an act of sexual intercourse) and with a Yevamah/widow of his deceased brother who died without issue, betrothal thorugh money is not a complete Kiddushin/betrothal, but rather, from the words of the Scribes/Soferim, the Yevamah is not acquired to the levir to become like a completely married woman until he comes upon her and he didn’t have sufficient time to marry her until he died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:8)


**שְׁנִיָּה חוֹלֶצֶת.** וְלֹא מַפְטְרָה מִשּׁוּם צָרַת עֶרְוָה, דְּלָאו צָרָתָהּ מַמָּשׁ הִיא:


שנייה חולצת – and it does not exempt her because of being a rival of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity because she is not actually her rival/co-wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:1:9)


**וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** מִשּׁוּם דְּמַאֲמָר קוֹנֶה מִקְצָת וְהַוְיָא לָהּ צָרַת עֶרְוָה בְּמִקְצָת. וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין שָׁם קִדּוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת:


ולא מתיבמת – because the statement of intention acquires partially and she is [also] partially a rival of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity, and in every place where there isn’t complete Kiddushin/betrothal, she performs the act of Halitzah and does not engage in a levirate marriage.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:2:1)


**וָמֵת.** הַמְיַבֵּם:


ומת – the levir who married the wife of the brother who died without issue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:2:2)


**מְיַבֵּם לְאֵיזוֹ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה.** וּפוֹטֶרֶת חֲבֶרְתָּהּ. וְלָאו אַעָשָׂה בָּהּ מַאֲמָר קָאֵי, אֶלָּא אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי דְּקָתָנֵי הָרִאשׁוֹנָה אֲסוּרָה וְכוּ', וְקָאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן כֵּיוָן שֶׁכְּשֶׁנּוֹלַד כְּבָר נִתְיַבְּמָה, וְלֹא הָיְתָה עַל זֶה בְּזִיקַת נִשּׂוּאֵי אָחִיו הָרִאשׁוֹן מֵעוֹלָם, מֻתֶּרֶת לוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


מיבם לאיזו שירצה – and she exempts her [co-wife] colleague, and this does not refer to performing a statement of intention [of levirate marriage] , but rather, it refers to the first clause [of the Mishnah], as it is taught, the first is prohibited, etc. and as Rabbi Shimon states for since he was born, she had already had a levirate marriage performed on her and she did not have on this one the levirate relation through the marriage of his first brother ever, she is permitted to him, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:3:1)


**לֹא חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** צָרָתָהּ, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הִיא. וְכֵן אַיְלוֹנִית, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) אֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵד, פְּרָט לְאַיְלוֹנִית. וְכֵן אֵשֶׁת סְרִיס חַמָּה וְאַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס, לֹא חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וּבֵן אֵין לוֹ, פְּרָט לְאֵלּוּ שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם בָּנִים. וְאֵשֶׁת גֵּר וְאֵשֶׁת עֶבֶד מְשֻׁחְרָר, לֹא חוֹלְצוֹת וְלֹא מִתְיַבְּמוֹת, שֶׁאֵין אַחֲוָה לַגֵּרִים וְלֹא לָעֲבָדִים. אֲבָל עֲקָרָה וּזְקֵנָה, אוֹ חוֹלְצוֹת אוֹ מִתְיַבְּמוֹת:


לא חולצת ולא מתיבמת – her rival/co-wife and all the more so she herself, and similarly, a barren woman, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:6): “[The first son] that she bears [shall be accounted to the dead brother that his name not be blotted out in Israel],” excluding a barren woman [ and similarly, the wife of a eunuch born without visible testicles and an androgynous individual does not engage in Halitzah nor levirate marriage, as it states ( there -Deuteronomy 25:5): “[When brothers dwell together and one of them lies] and leaves no son, [the wife of the deceased shall not be married to a stranger, outside the family],” excluding those for whom it is inappropriate for them to have children, and the wife of a convert and the wife of a freed slave does not engage in Halitzah nor levirate marriage, for there is no legal status of consanguinity neither for converts nor slaves, but a barren woman and an elderly women either engages in Halitzah or levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:3:2)


**אֲחוֹתָהּ.** שֶׁל עֶרְוָה: **כְּשֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתָּהּ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי אֲחָיוֹת מִשְּׁנֵי אֶחָיו, וְהָאַחַת אֲסוּרָה עָלָיו מִשּׁוּם עֶרְוָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִיא חֲמוֹתוֹ אוֹ כַּלָּתוֹ: **חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֹא פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דְּעֶרְוָה לָאו זְקוּקָתוֹ הִיא:


אחותה – [her sister] who is a woman forbidden to a man on account of consanguinity when she is [also] her sister-in-law [widow of her childless brother-in-law] such as, for example, when two sisters fell to him from his two brothers and the one is prohibited upon him because of consanguinity such as when she is his mother-in-law or daughter-in-law, she performs Halitzah or levirate marriage but now did not come in contact with the sister of his levirate relation because someone forbidden on account of consanguinity is not his levirate relation.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:1)


**שְׁנִיּוֹת.** לָעֲרָיוֹת. שֶׁגָּזְרוּ סוֹפְרִים עֲלֵיהֶם. וְנִקְרְאוּ אִסּוּר מִצְוָה, שֶׁמִּצְוָה לִשְׁמֹעַ דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים. וְאֵלּוּ הֵן, אֵם אִמּוֹ וְאֵין לָהּ הֶפְסֵק, וְאֵם אֲבִי אִמּוֹ בִּלְבַד, וְאֵם אָבִיו וְאֵין לָהּ הֶפְסֵק, [וְאֵם אֲבִי אָבִיו], וְאֵשֶׁת אֲבִי אָבִיו וְאֵין לָהּ הֶפְסֵק, וְאֵשֶׁת אֲבִי אִמּוֹ בִּלְבַד, וְאֵשֶׁת אֲחִי הָאָב מִן הָאֵם, וְאֵשֶׁת אֲחִי הָאֵם בֵּין מִן הָאֵם בֵּין מִן הָאָב, וְכַלַּת בְּנוֹ וְאֵין לָהּ הֶפְסֵק, וְכַלַּת בִּתּוֹ, וּבַת בַּת בְּנוֹ, וּבַת בַּת בִּתּוֹ, וּבַת בֶּן בְּנוֹ, וּבַת בֶּן בִּתּוֹ, וּבַת בַּת בֶּן אִשְׁתּוֹ, וּבַת בַּת בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְאֵם אֵם אֲבִי אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְאֵם אֵם אֵם אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְאֵם אַב אֵם אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְאֵם אַב אֲבִי אִשְׁתּוֹ:


שניות – [secondary grade of forbidden relationships seen as incestuous] – forbidden incestuous or adulterous sexual relationships that the Scribes/Soferim decreed upon them which are called prohibitions resulting from a Mitzvah (i.e., based upon the Mitzvah not to deviate from the rulings of the Sages – see Deuteronomy 17:11), for it is a Mitzvah to hearken to the words of the Sages, and which are they? The mother of his mother, and there is no limitation but if it is the father of his mother alone, and the mother of his father, and it has no limitation [and if the father of his father] and the wife of his father’s father, and it has no limitation, and the wife of his mother’s father alone, and the wife of his brother’s father from the mother, and the wife of his mother’s brother, whether from the mother or whether from the father and there is no limitation, and daughter-in-law of his daughter, and the daughter of his son’s daughter and the daughter of the daughter’s daughter and the daughter of’s his son’s son and the daughter of his son’s daughter, and the daughter [of the daughter] of the son of his wife, and the daughter [of the daughter] of his wife’s daughter, and the mother of the mother of his father’s wife, and the mother of [the mother] of his wife’s mother, and the father of the mother of his wife’s mother, and the mother of the father of his father’s wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:2)


**אַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** שֶׁמֵּת אָחִיו הַהֶדְיוֹט וְנָפְלָה אִשְׁתּוֹ לְפָנָיו:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:4:3)


**גְּרוּשָׁה וַחֲלוּצָה לְכֹהֵן הֶדְיוֹט.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁעָבַר אָחִיו הַמֵּת וְנָשָׂא גְּרוּשָׁה וַחֲלוּצָה, וּכְשֶׁמֵּת צְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי תָּפְסוּ לוֹ בָּהּ קִדּוּשִׁין מִשּׁוּם דְּקִדּוּשִׁין תּוֹפְסִין בְּחַיָּבֵי לָאוִין. אֲבָל יִבּוּמֵי לֹא, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא אֲסוּרָה לוֹ. וּלְפָטְרָהּ בְּלֹא כְלוּם אִי אֶפְשָׁר, דְּלֹא אַלִּים לָאו לְמִפְטְרָה, דְּיָלְפִינַן לָהּ מֵאֲחוֹת אִשָּׁה שֶׁהִיא בְּכָרֵת:


גרושה וחלוצה לכהן הדיוט – such as, for example, that he (i.e., as a Kohen) transgressed his dead brother and had married a divorcee or a woman who had undergone Halitzah, and when he died, she needs Halitzah for Kiddushin/betrothal takes effect in those liable for violating negative commandments, but not levirate marriage, for she is prohibited to him, but to exempt/free her without anything is impossible, for it is not strong to release her, for we derive “her” from the wife’s sister, which is punishable by extirpation.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:1)


**אָח מִכָּל מָקוֹם.** וַאֲפִלּוּ מַמְזֵר:


אח מכל מקום – even a Mamzer/illegitimate child (see Talmud Yevamot 22a)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:2)


**אָחִיו לְכָל דָּבָר.** לְיוֹרְשׁוֹ וְלְהִטָּמֵא לוֹ:


אחיו לכל דבר – to inherit him and to become defiled to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:5:3)


**שִׁפְחָה וְנָכְרִית.** וְלָדָהּ בַּתְרָהּ אָזִיל, דִּכְתִיב בְּשִׁפְחָה (שמות כא) הָאִשָּׁה וִילָדֶיהָ תִּהְיֶה לַאדֹנֶיהָ. וּבְנָכְרִית כְּתִיב (דברים ז) כִּי יָסִיר אֶת בִּנְךָ מֵאַחֲרַי, וּמִדְּלֹא כְתִיב כִּי תָסִיר, שְׁמַע מִנָּה הָכִי קָאָמַר, בִּתְּךָ לֹא תִתֵּן לִבְנוֹ, כִּי יָסִיר בַּעַל בִּתְּךָ אֶת בִּנְךָ שֶׁתֵּלֵד לוֹ בִּתְּךָ מֵאַחֲרַי, אֲבָל אַבִּתּוֹ לֹא תִּקַּח לְבִנְךָ לֹא מְהַדַּר, שֶׁאֵין הַבֵּן הַבָּא מִן הַנָּכְרִית קָרוּי בִּנְךָ אֶלָּא בְּנָהּ:


שפחה נכרית – her offspring we got after her, as it is written regarding the maidservant (Exodus 21:4): “[If his master gave him a wife, and she has borne him children,] the wife and her children shall belong to the master, [and he shall leave alone],” but regarding a heathen woman, it is written (Deuteronomy 7:4): “For they (literally, “he) will turn your children away from Me [to worship other gods],” since it is not written, “for you will turn [your children] away,” we learn from it - this is what he said (Deuteronomy 7:4): “Do not give your daughters to their sons” – for the husband of your daughter will turn aside the son that your daughter will give birth to him away from Me. But regarding “or take their daughters for your sons” (Deuteronomy 7:3), we are not zealous in religious observance, for a child that comes from the heathen woman is not called your son, but rather, her son.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:1)


**וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ אֵיזוֹ קִדֵּשׁ.** וְאָסוּר בִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן, מִשּׁוּם דְּכָל חֲדָא וַחֲדָא אִיכָּא לְמֵימַר זוֹ אֲחוֹת אִשָּׁה:


ואינו יודע איזו קדש – and he is prohibited to both of them because each one of them is able to say, this is the wife’s sister.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:2)


**חוֹלֵץ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן.** דְּלֹא יָדַע אֵיזוֹ הִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ. וּמִחְלַץ לַחֲדָא וּלְיִבּוּמֵי לְאִידָךְ, לֹא אֶפְשָׁר, דַּאֲחוֹת חֲלוּצָתוֹ הִיא, וַאֲסוּרָה לוֹ מִדְּרַבָּנָן בְּחַיֶּיהָ. וְיִבּוּמֵי בְּרֵישָׁא נַמִּי לֹא, דְּדִלְמָא לֹא זוֹ הִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ וְקָפָגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ דְּהַוְיָא כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ:


חולץ לשתיהן – because she is the sister of the woman engaged in Halitzah, and she is prohibited to him [during her lifetime] according to the Rabbis, but also not to perform levirate marriage in the first clause. For perhaps this is not his widow of the deceased husband who died without issue, and when he came in contact with the sister of his levirate relation, it is like she is his wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:3)


**הָיוּ לוֹ שְׁנַיִם, אֶחָד חוֹלֵץ.** תְּחִלָּה לָאַחַת, וְהַשֵּׁנִי אִם רָצָה לְיַבֵּם לַשְּׁנִיָּה מְיַבֵּם, שֶׁאִם זוֹ יְבִמְתּוֹ שַׁפִּיר, וְאִם אֲחוֹתָהּ הִיא, לֹא פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דַּחֲלִיצָה דְּהַךְ אַפְקַעְתָּא לַזִּקָּה:


היו לו שנים אחד חולץ – at first to the first [woman] and the second, if he wants to engage in levirate marriage with the second, he does so, for if this [one] is his deceased brother’s widow, fine, and if she is her sister, he did not approach the sister of his levirate relation because Halitzah for that one removes her from being a levirate relation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:4)


**קָדְמוּ וְכָנְסוּ.** קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּמְלְכוּ בְּבֵית דִּין:


קדמו וכנסו – prior to the Jewish court took counsel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:6:5)


**אֵין מוֹצִיאִין אוֹתָן מִיָּדָם.** מִשּׁוּם דְּכָל חַד וְחַד אָמַר, אֲנָא שַׁפִּיר נַסְבֵי. וְאִי נַמִּי הֲוֵי אִיפְּכָא, שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁכָּנַס לְיִבּוּם כָּנַס אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ, הַהִיא שַׁעְתָּא הוּא דְּעָבַד אִסּוּרָא, וּמִשֶּׁבָּא אָחִיו וְיִבֵּם אֶת הַשְּׁנִיָּה שֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ, פַּקְעָא זִקָּתָהּ לְמַפְרֵעַ מֵאִידָךְ:


אין מוציאין אותן מידם – that we don’t remove them from their hands because each one said I am best to marry; alternatively, it is the opposite, that the first who performed a levirate marriage, married the sister of his levirate relation, at that hour he performed a prohibited action and when his brother came and performed a levirate marriage with the second, who is the widow of the deceased brother who died without issue, her levirate relationship has ceased retroactively from the other one.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:1)


**שְׁנַיִם שֶׁקִּדְּשׁוּ שְׁתֵּי אֲחָיוֹת.** וְהֵם נָכְרִים:


שנים שקדשו שתי אחיות – two strangers (i.e., not related to each other).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:2)


**זֶה חוֹלֵץ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן כוּ'.** מִשּׁוּם דְּכָל חַד מִנַּיהוּ אָסוּר לְיִבּוּמֵי, דְּדִלְמָא כָּל חַד וְחַד פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ:


זה חולץ לשתיהן כו' – because each of them is prohibited to perform levirate marriages for perhaps eah one of them came in contact with the sister of his levirate connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:3)


**הַיָּחִיד חוֹלֵץ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ לְיַבֵּם, לֹא קֹדֶם חֲלִיצָה שֶׁל שְׁנִיָּה מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה, וְלֹא לְאַחַר חֲלִיצָה מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת חֲלוּצָה. לְפִיכָךְ הוֹאִיל וְאָסוּר לְיַבֵּם יַחְלֹץ תְּחִלָּה לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן, כְּדֵי לְהוֹצִיא אוֹתָהּ שֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ מֵאִסּוּר יְבָמָה לַשּׁוּק:


היחיד חולץ לשתיהן – for it is impossible for him to perform levirate marriage not prior to the Halitzah of the second because of [the prohibition of] the sister of his levirate relation, but not after Halitzah because of the sister of the woman undergoing Halitzah; therefore, since it is prohibited to perform levirate marriage, he should perform Halitzah to both of them in order to remove her [i.e., the woman] who is his Yevamah /widow of her deceased husband who died without issue from the prohibition of a woman who is the widow of a deceased husband who died without issue from the general public.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:4)


**וְהַשְּׁנַיִם אֶחָד חוֹלֵץ.** לְאַפְקוּעֵי זִקָּה מֵאָחִיו אִם זוֹ יְבִמְתּוֹ, וְתִשְׁתְּרֵי אֲחוֹתָהּ לַשֵּׁנִי מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ, אִי יְבִמְתּוֹ הִיא, שַׁפִּיר, וְאִי אֲחוֹתָהּ הִיא, הָא פָּקְעָה זִקַּת הַיְּבָמָה מִנֵּיהּ בַּחֲלִיצַת אָחִיו:


והשנים אחד חולץ – to cause a release of the levirate relation from his brother, if this woman is his Yevama/widow of his dead brother who died without issue, and permit her sister to the second [brother] whatever you want, if she is his sister-in-law/Yevamah, fine, and if she is her sister, you have released the levirate relation of the sister-in-law from him through the ac of Halitzah by his brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:5)


**אָחִיו שֶׁל זֶה מְיַבֵּם חֲלוּצָתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה.** מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ, אִם זוֹ יְבִמְתּוֹ שַׁפִּיר נָסִיב, שֶׁהֲרֵי אָחִיו לֹא חָלַץ לְזוֹ אֶלָּא לַאֲחוֹתָהּ שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה יְבִמְתּוֹ וְאֵינָהּ כְּלוּם. וְאִי לָאו יְבִמְתּוֹ הִיא שַׁפִּיר נָסִיב, נָכְרִיָּה הִיא, דְּאִי מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה לֵיכָּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי חָלַץ אָחִיו אֶת אֲחוֹתָהּ שֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ וּפָקְעָה זִקָּה. וְאִי מִשּׁוּם יְבָמָה לַשּׁוּק, הֲרֵי חָלְצָה זוֹ מֵאֲחִי בַּעֲלָהּ:


אחיו של זה מייבם חלוצתו של זה – whatever you want if this is his sister-in-law, he marries her and it is well and good, for his brother did not perform Halitzah on that one but rather on her sister, who was not his sister-in-law and is not anything [to him] and if she is not his sister-in-law , he has done well by marrying a stranger (i.e., unconnected woman), for if it were because she is the sister of the levirate relation, it is nothing, since his brother performed on Halitzah with her sister who is his sister-in-law/Yevamah, and the levirate relation of the sister-in-law has been released, but if it was because it was an entrance to the open marketplace, this one has performed Halitzah with the brothers of her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:6)


**קָדְמוּ שְׁנֵי אַחִין.** שֶׁל אֶחָד וְחָלְצוּ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא יָדְעֵי אֵיזוֹ הִיא יְבִמְתּוֹ:


קדמו שני אחין – of one of them and they performed Halitzah with both of them (i.e., the women) to his a


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:7)


**לֹא יְיַבְּמוּ.** שְׁנֵי אַחִים הָאֲחֵרִים זֶה אֶת זוֹ וְזֶה אֶת זוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דְּקַמָּא דְּנָסִיב אִית לֵיהּ לְמֵימַר זוֹ הִיא אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ: **אֶלָּא אֶחָד חוֹלֵץ.** תְּחִלָּה, לָאַחַת:


לא ייבמו – the two [other] brothers, one with this [woman] and that one (i.e., brother) with the other [woman] because the first one that marries, is able to say, this woman is the sister of his levirate relation, but rather, one [brother] performs Halitzah first to one of


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:8)


**וְאֶחָד מְיַבֵּם.** לַשְּׁנִיָּה, מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ, אִי יְבִמְתּוֹ הִיא שַׁפִּיר, וְאִי לָאו, בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה לֹא פָּגַע, שֶׁהֲרֵי חָלַץ אָחִיו לִיבִמְתּוֹ. וִיבָמָה לַשּׁוּק לֹא הֲוֵי, שֶׁהֲרֵי חָלְצָה מֵאֲחִי בַּעֲלָהּ:


ואחד מיבם – and the other [brother] performs levirate marriage with the second, whatever you want, if he is sister-in-law, fine, and if not, he has not come in contact with the sister of his levirate relation for his brother performed Halitzah and a sister-in-law in the free market does not happen, for she underwent Halitzah from the brothers of her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:7:9)


**וְאִם קָדְמוּ.** אֵלּוּ שְׁנַיִם אַחֲרוֹנִים וְכָנְסוּ אַחַר חֲלִיצַת שְׁנַיִם הָאֲחֵרִים וְלֹא בָּאוּ לִמָּלֵךְ בְּבֵית דִּין, אֵין מוֹצִיאִין מִיָּדָן, כְּדִפְרִישִׁית, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין כָּאן אֶלָּא סְפֵק אִסּוּר אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה בְּנִשּׂוּאֵי רִאשׁוֹנָה, וְאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר קַמָּא דְּנָסִיב יְבִמְתּוֹ הִיא וְשַׁפִּיר נָסִיב, וּבַתְרָא נַמִּי שַׁפִּיר נָסִיב נָכְרִיָּה, וְאִי נַמִּי הֲוֵי אִינְסִיבָא אִיפְּכָא וְקַמָּא פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁבָּא שֵׁנִי וְיִבֵּם פָּקְעָה זִקָּתָהּ מֵאָחִיו וְהֻתְּרָה לוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְאִסּוּרָא דְּעָבַד עָבַד:


ואם קדמו – these latter two [brothers] and married after the Halitzah of the two other [women] and they did not come to take council with the Jewish court, we don’t remove them from their hands as we have explained, for here is nothing here other than a doubtful prohibition of the sister of one’s levirate relation om the first marriage, and one can state that the first that married his sister-in-law and it is well tha the married, and the latter, also did well by marrying strange (i.e., unattached) woman, or alternatively, if if they married in reverse, that the father-in-law came in contact with the sister of his levirate relation and since he second [brother] came and performed levirate marriage, her levirate connection has been severed from his brother and she is permitted to him as his wife, but the prohibition that he performed was performed.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:1)


**מִצְוָה בַגָּדוֹל לְיַבֵּם.** דְּדָרְשִׁינַן וּלְקָחָהּ לוֹ לְאִשָּׁה וְיִבְּמָהּ וְהָיָה הַבְּכוֹר, הַמְיַבֵּם יְהֵא בְּכוֹר. אֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵד, וְאוֹתָהּ יְבָמָה תְּהֵא רְאוּיָה לֵילֵד, פְּרָט לְאַיְלוֹנִית. יָקוּם הַבְּכוֹר הַיָּבָם עַל שֵׁם אָחִיו לִנְחֹל אוֹתוֹ, וְלֹא יַחְלְקוּ אֶחָיו עִמּוֹ:


מצוה בגדול ליבם – for we expound (Deuteronomy 25:5-6): “he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty. The first son/והיה הבכור that she bears [shall be accounted to the dead brother [that his name may not be blotted out in Israel],” that the person who performs the levirate marriage should be a first-born, “that she bears” and that sister-in-law should be worthy to give birth, excluding a barren woman; יקום/shall be accounted – the first born who performs as the levir in the name of his brother to cause him to inherit and not that his brothers divide it with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:2)


**הַנִּטְעָן עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ.** הֶחָשׁוּד עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ:


הנטען על אשת איש – who is suspected of [having a sexual relationship with] a married woman


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:3)


**וְהוֹצִיאוּהָ.** בֵּית דִּין מִתַּחַת בַּעֲלָהּ, בִּשְׁבִיל זֶה שֶׁאֲסָרָהּ עָלָיו, וְהָלַךְ זֶה וּנְשָׂאָהּ:


והוציאוה – and the Jewish court removed her from under her husband, for that reason that she is prohibited to him and this one went and married her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:8:4)


**יוֹצִיא.** דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא אֲסוּרָה נַמִּי לוֹ כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֲסוּרָה לַבַּעַל, כִּדְדָרְשִׁינַן מִנִּטְמָאָה נִטְמָאָה שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים, אֶחָד לַבַּעַל וְאֶחָד לַבּוֹעֵל. וְדַוְקָא הַנִּטְעָן עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ, אֲבָל נִטְעָן עַל הַפְּנוּיָה, נִרְאֶה דְּמִצְוָה לִכְנֹס, כִּדְאַשְׁכְּחָן גַּבֵּי אוֹנֵס (דברים כב) וְלוֹ תִּהְיֶה לְאִשָּׁה:


יוציא – that from the Torah, she is forbidden also to him, as she is forbidden to the husband, as we expounded, that once she is defiled, she is defiled twice – once to the husband and the other time to the person engaged in sexual relations wither. And specifically one who is suspected [of having intercourse] with a married woman, but one who is suspected [of having intercourse] with a free/unattached woman, it appears that it is Mitzvah to marry [her] as we find concerning rape (Deuteronomy 22:19): “Moreover, she shall remain is wife; [he shall never have the right to divorce her].”



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא גֵט מִמְּדִינַת הַיָּם.** צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתַּם. וְטַעְמָא מְפֹרָשׁ בְּגִטִּין, לְמַר מִשּׁוּם שֶׁאֵין בְּקִיאִין לִשְׁמָהּ, וּלְמַר שֶׁאֵין עֵדִים מְצוּיִין לְקַיְּמוֹ:


המביא גט ממדינת הים – he must state, “in my presence it was written and in my presence it was signed,” and the reason is explained in [Tractate] Gittin (see Bartenura commentary to Chapter 1, Mishnah 1) for [according to] one Master, because they are not expert in [writing the Jewish bill of divorce] for her sake, and to the [other] Master, because witnesses are not found to attest to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:2)


**לֹא יִשָּׂא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאַדִּבּוּרֵיהּ סָמְכִינַן:


לא ישא את אשתו – because we rely upon his word.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:3)


**מֵת הֲרַגְתִּיו וְכוּ'.** הוֹאִיל וְעַל עֵדוּתוֹ נִשֵּׂאת, אִיכָּא לְזוּת שְׂפָתַיִם, שֶׁמָּא עֵינָיו נָתַן בָּהּ וְהֵעִיד עֵדוּת שֶׁקֶר עָלֶיהָ. אֲבָל תִּנָּשֵׂא לְאַחֵר, דְּאִשָּׁה נִשֵּׂאת בְּעֵד אֶחָד:


מת הרגתיו וכו' – since it was upon his testimony that she married, and it is possible to slander, lest his eyes were upon her and he made a false testimony about her but she could marry to another, for a woman can be married [upon the testimony] of one witness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר הֲרַגְתִּיו לֹא תִנָּשֵׂא אִשְׁתּוֹ.** בְּעֵדוּתוֹ אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחֵר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה אַל תָּשֵׁת רָשָׁע עֵד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, דְּקַיְמָא לָן אֵין אָדָם מֵשִׂים עַצְמוֹ רָשָׁע, דְּאָדָם קָרוֹב אֵצֶל עַצְמוֹ. וּמְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג, אֲבָל לֹא מְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ בְּמַאי דְּאָמַר שֶׁהוּא הֲרָגוֹ. וְהִלְכָּךְ אֲפִלּוּ כִּי אָמַר הֲרַגְתִּיו תִּנָּשֵׂא אִשְׁתּוֹ:


ר"י אומר הרגתיו לא תנשא אשתו – through his testimony even to another because he is a wicked person, and the Torah stated (Exodus 23:1): “You shall not join hands with the guilty [to act as a malicious] witness,” but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda, for we hold that a person does not incriminate himself (i.e., his testimony against himself has no legal effect -see Tractate Sanhedrin 9b), for a person is considered a relative regarding himself, and we believe him that that he was killed, but we don’t believe him regarding what he said that he killed him, and therefore, even if he said that he killed him, you can marry his wife.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:9:5)


**הֲרַגְנוּהוּ תִּנָּשֵׂא אִשְׁתּוֹ.** מְפֹרָשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, בְּאוֹמֵר אֲנִי הָיִיתִי עִם הוֹרְגָיו אֲבָל אֲנִי לֹא הֲרַגְתִּיו:


הרגנוהו תנשא אשתו – it is explained in the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 25b) when he says: I was with those who killed him, but I did not kill him.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:1)


**בְּנֶדֶר.** שֶׁנָּדְרָה הֲנָאָה מִבַּעֲלָהּ וְלֹא הֵפֵר לָהּ, וּבָאת לְחָכָם לְהַתִּיר לָהּ וְלֹא מָצָא פֶּתַח לַחֲרָטָה:


בנדר – she took a vow refusing any benefit/favor from her husband and he did not and he did not invalidate it and she came to a Sage to release her and he did not find an opening for regret for a vow made under misapprehension.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:2)


**לֹא יִשָּׂאֶנָּה.** מִשּׁוּם חֲשָׁד:


לא ישאנה – because of suspicion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:3)


**שֶׁהוּא בֵית דִּין.** כְּלוֹמַר, מֵאוּן אוֹ חֲלִיצָה לֹא עָשָׂה חָכָם זֶה בִּיחִידִי, מִשּׁוּם דְּקַיְמָא לָן חֲלִיצָה וּמֵאוּנִים בִּשְׁנַיִם וּשְׁלֹשָׁה לֹא חֲשִׁידֵי. אֲבָל הֶתֵּר נְדָרִים בְּיָחִיד מֻמְחֶה:


שהוא ב"ד – meaning to say, a woman’s protest against a marriage contracted during her minority and Halitzah – this Sage did not do as an individual because we hold that Halitzah and a woman’s protest against a marriage contracted during her minority [we require a court] of two or three we don’t suspect, but the release from vows [is performed] by an expert individual.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:4)


**וְכֻלָּן.** הֶחָכָם וְהַמֵּבִיא גֵּט וְהַמֵּעִיד עַל הָאִשָּׁה לְהַשִּׂיאָהּ, דִּתְנַן בְּהוּ לֹא יִכְנֹס, אִם הָיוּ לָהֶן נָשִׁים בִּשְׁעַת מַעֲשֶׂה, וָמֵתוּ אַחַר כָּךְ, מֻתָּרוֹת לְהִנָּשֵׂא לָהֶם דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֵיכָּא חֲשָׁד:


וכולן – the Sage and he person bringing a Jewish bill of divorce and a person testifying about a woman to [be allowed] to marry her, as are taught about in the Mishnah, he should not take her in marriage if he had wives at the time of the action but if they died afterwards, they (i.e., the women) are permitted to marry them for now there is no suspicion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:5)


**וְכֻלָּן.** הַנָּשִׁים הַלָּלוּ:


וכולן – these women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:6)


**שֶׁנִּשְּׂאוּ לַאֲחֵרִים.** כְּשֶׁאֲסָרָהּ הֶחָכָם אוֹ כְּשֶׁהֵעִיד הָעֵד שֶׁמֵּת בַּעֲלָהּ, וּמֵתוּ הַבְּעָלִים הַשְּׁנִיִּים:


שנישאו לאחרים – that the Sage forbade or to the witness or to the person who brings the Jewish bill of divorce [from abroad].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:7)


**מֻתָּרוֹת לִנָּשֵׂא.** לֶחָכָם וְלָעֵד וְלַמֵּבִיא גֵּט:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 2:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/2:10:8)


**וְכֻלָּן מֻתָּרוֹת לִבְנֵיהֶם וְלַאֲחֵיהֶן.** שֶׁל אֵלּוּ הַמַּתִּירִים אוֹתָן. וְאֵינָן אֲסוּרוֹת אֶלָּא לָהֶם לְבַדָּם, דְּאֵין אָדָם חוֹטֵא בִּשְׁבִיל בְּנוֹ אוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל אָחִיו. וְכָל הָנֵי דְּאָמְרִינַן לֹא יִכְנֹס, אִם כָּנַס אֵינוֹ מוֹצִיא, חוּץ מֵהַנִּטְעָן עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ, שֶׁאִם הוֹצִיאוּהָ בֵּית דִּין מִתַּחַת בַּעֲלָהּ בְּעֵדִים וּרְאָיָה בְּרוּרָה, אֲפִלּוּ אִם כָּנַס יוֹצִיא. וְאִם בָּאוּ עֵדֵי דָּבָר מְכֹעָר בִּלְבַד, כְּגוֹן שֶׁרָאוּ אָדָם יוֹצֵא וְהִיא חוֹגֶרֶת בְּסִנָּר אוֹ אָדָם יוֹצֵא וְהִיא עוֹמֶדֶת מֵעַל הַמִּטָּה וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה, אִם כָּנַס לֹא יוֹצִיא. וְאִם לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּאוּ עֵדֵי דָּבָר מְכֹעָר יָצָא קָלָא וְלֹא פָּסִיק, שֶׁעָמַד הַקּוֹל יוֹם וָחֵצִי, אִם כָּנַס יוֹצִיא, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיוּ לוֹ בָּנִים מִמֶּנָּה, שֶׁאָז אֵינוֹ מוֹצִיא כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא לְהוֹצִיא לַעַז עַל בָּנָיו:


וכולן מותרות לבניהם ולאחיהן – of these who permit them and they are not forbidden other than to them alone, for a man does not sin for his son or for his brothers, and all those that we mentioned, one should not marry, and if he married, he should not remove/divorce except for the where there is a claim that she I s married woman, for if the Jewish court removed her from her husband with witness and clear proof, even if he married her, he should remove/divorce her. But if witnesses came regarding an ugly matter alone, such as that they saw a man going out and she is wearing [only] a petticoat/breech-cloth (and Ezra, according to Talmud Bava Kamma 82a ordained that a woman must wear such clothing as a matter of chastity), or a man goes out and she is standing on top of the bed and similar kinds of things, if he married her, he cannot remove/divorce her, and if after the witnesses about an ugly matter came and a rumor went out and didn’t cease for the rumor stood for a day-and-a-half, if he married her, he should divorce her, other than if they had children from her, for then, he doesn’t remove her in order to not to cast aspersion upon his children.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:1:1)


**אַרְבָּעָה אַחִין.** חוֹלְצוֹת וְלֹא מִתְיַבְּמוֹת. דְּכֵיוָן דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ זְקוּקִין לְהַאי וּלְהַאי, קַמָּא דִּמְיַבֵּם פָּגַע בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ דְּהַוְיָא כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ:


ארבעה אחין. חולצות ולא מיבמות – since both of them are levirate relations one to the other. The first [brother] who undergoes levirate marriage came in contact with the sister of his levirate relation and is like his wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:1:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יְקַיְּמוּ וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יוֹצִיאוּ.** בַּגְּמָרָא מַפְּכִינַן לָהּ, בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יוֹצִיאוּ וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יְקַיְּמוּ. דִּבְכָל מָקוֹם בֵּית שַׁמַּאי לְחֻמְרָא וּבֵית הִלֵּל לְקֻלָּא חוּץ מֵהַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַיְדוּעִים מִקֻּלֵּי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּמֵחֻמְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל. וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בְּעָלְמָא, בֵּית שַׁמַּאי בִּמְקוֹם בֵּית הִלֵּל אֵינָהּ מִשְׁנָה, כְּלוֹמַר, כָּל הֵיכָא דְּאַשְׁכְּחָן בַּמִּשְׁנָה בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְקִילִין, וּבֵית הִלֵּל הוּא שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהָקֵל וְנִמְצְאוּ עַתָּה בֵּית שַׁמַּאי עוֹמְדִים בִּמְקוֹם בֵּית הִלֵּל, אֵינָהּ מִשְׁנָה וּמְשַׁבַּשְׁתָּא הִיא וְצָרִיךְ לְהָפְכָהּ, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִיא מֵהַמִּשְׁנָיוֹת הַיְּדוּעוֹת שֶׁהֵם מִקֻּלֵּי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּמֵחֻמְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת עֵדֻיּוֹת מָנֵי לָהּ בְּשֵׁם רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מִקֻּלֵּי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּמֵחֻמְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל:


ב"ש אומרי יקיימו ובה"א יוציאו – In the Gemara (Talmud Yevamot 28a) reverses them that the School of Shammai states that they should be put away (i.e., divorced), and the School of Hillel states that they may remain wedded, for in every place, the School of Shammai is stringent and the School of Hillel is lenient except from those known places where the School of Shammai is more lenient and the School of Hillel is more stringent (see, for example, the first five Mishnayot in Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 5, of which this text is taken), and thus we say, that generally, the School of SHammai in the place of the Schol of Hillel is not a Mishnah, meaning to say, that wherever we find in the Mishnah that the School of Shammai are lenient and the School of Hillel, where it is their manner to be lenient, but and it is found now that the School of Shammai stands in the place of the School of Hillel, it is not a Mishnah and it is a corrupted text and it is necessary to reverse it unless it is from the known Mishnayot which are from the leniencies of the School of Shammai and the stringencies of the School of Hillel. And in Tractate Eduyot (Chapter 5, Mishnah 5), in the name of Rabbi Eliezer, it enumerates the leniencies of the School of Shammai and the stringencies of the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:1)


**אִסּוּר עֶרְוָה.** כְּגוֹן חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חֲמוֹתוֹ:


איסור ערוה – such as, for example, his mother-in-law and the mother of his mother-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:2)


**וּמֻתָּר בַּאֲחוֹתָהּ.** דְּלָאו אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ הִיא, דְּעֶרְוָה לֹא רַמְיָא קַמֵּיהּ לְיִבּוּמֵי:


ומותר באחותה – for she is not the sister of his levirate relation for a woman forbidden to a man on account of consanguinity is not lying before him for levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:2:3)


**אִסּוּר מִצְוָה.** רַמְיָא קַמֵּיהּ מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, הִלְכָּךְ אָסוּר בַּאֲחוֹתָהּ, דַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ הִיא:


איסור מצוה – lying before him from the Torah, therefore, he is forbidden to her sister, for she is the sister of his levirate relation.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:3:1)


**אֲחוֹתָהּ כְּשֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתָּהּ.** אֲחוֹתָהּ שֶׁל עֶרְוָה כְּשֶׁהִיא יְבִמְתָּהּ אֵשֶׁת אֲחִי בַּעֲלָהּ, כְּשֶׁנּוֹפֶלֶת עִמָּהּ לְיִבּוּם:


אחותו כשהיא יבמתו – her sister of a woman forbidden to a man on account of consanguinity, when she is his sister-in-law, the wife of her husband’s brother when she falls [with her] for levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:3:2)


**אוֹ חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** דְּלָאו אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָתוֹ הִיא, דְּעֶרְוָה לֹא רַמְיָא קַמֵּיהּ:


או חולצת או מתיבמת – for she is not the sister of his levirate relation for there is no prohibition on account of consanguinity is not lying before him (see also Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 3).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:4:1)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר.** טַעֲמָא דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יח) וְאִשָּׁה אֶל אֲחוֹתָהּ לֹא תִקַּח לִצְרֹר, בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ צָרוֹת זוֹ לָזוֹ, כְּגוֹן הָכָא שֶׁשְּׁתֵּיהֶן זְקוּקוֹת לוֹ לְיִבּוּם, לֹא יְהֵא לְךָ לִקּוּחִין לֹא בָזוֹ וְלֹא בָזוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ור"ש פוטר – (see Tractate Yevamot 28b) – the reason of Rabbi Shimon as it is written (Leviticus 18:18): “Do not marry a woman as a rival to her sister [and uncover her nakedness in the other’s lifetime],” at the time that they are made rival/co-wives to each other, such as the example of here, where both of them are levirate relations to him for levirate marriage. You should not have a legal acquisition neither to this one nor to that one, and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:1)


**וְאֶחָד מֻפְנֶה.** בְּלֹא אִשָּׁה:


ואחד מופנה – without a wife (i.e., single, a bachelor).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:2)


**אִשְׁתּוֹ עִמּוֹ.** דְּקָסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי בַּעֲלַת מַאֲמָר הִיא חֲשׁוּבָה כִּכְנוּסָה, וּכְשֶׁנָּפְלָה אֲחוֹתָהּ לְאַחַר כֵּן לֹא מִתְּסְרָה מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה:


אשתו עמו – for since the School of Shammai holds that she is [his] wife through statement of intention [of levirate marriage] is considered as married, and when her sister fell after this [through the death of her husband, the second brother], she was not forbidden because of her being the sister of his levirate relation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:3)


**וְהַלָּה תֵצֵא.** אַף מִן הַחֲלִיצָה, מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת אִשָּׁה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:5:4)


**מוֹצִיא אִשְׁתּוֹ בְגֵט.** דְּלֹא אַלִּים מַאֲמָר לְשַׁוְיָהּ כִּכְנוּסָה, וְזוֹ אוֹסַרְתָּהּ מִשּׁוּם אֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה. וּצְרִיכָה גֵּט מִפְּנֵי הַמַּאֲמָר דַּהֲוֵי קִדּוּשִׁין בְּמִקְצָת וְלֹא פָּקְעֵי קִדּוּשִׁין בְּלֹא גֵּט, וּצְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה דְּהוֹאִיל וְלֹא הֲוֵי מַאֲמָר קִדּוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִין עֲדַיִן זְקוּקָתוֹ הִיא וּצְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה לְזִקָּתָהּ. וּבְרֵישָׁא יָהֵיב לָהּ גִּטָּא, וַהֲדַר חָלִיץ לָהּ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


מוציא אשתו בגט – for the statement of intention of [eventual] levirate marriage is not strong to compare it to someone married, and this prohibits her because of her being the sister of his levirate relation, and it requires a Jewish bill of divorce because the statement of intention which is partial betrothal/Kiddushin and Kiddushin is not released without a Jewish bill of divorce. And she requires Halitzah for since the statement of intention is not complete Kiddushin/betrothal, she is still his levirate relation and requires Halitzah for her levirate connection. And in the opening clause [of the Mishnah], he gives her a Jewish bill of divorce, and then performs Halitzah to her, and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:6:1)


**נָכְרִית.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ קְרוֹבָה לֹא לְזוֹ וְלֹא לְזוֹ:


נכרית – she is not related neither to this [woman] nor to that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:6:2)


**נָכְרִית חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** הוּא הַדִּין אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא עָשָׂה בָּהּ מַאֲמָר, וָמֵת, נָכְרִית מִחְלַץ חָלְצָה יִבּוּמֵי לֹא מְיַבְּמָהּ, דְּהַוְיָא לָהּ צָרַת אֲחוֹת אִשָּׁה בְּזִקָּה. וְהַאי דְּקָתָנֵי מַאֲמָר, לַאֲפוֹקֵי מִדְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי דְּאָמְרֵי מַאֲמָר קוֹנֶה קִנְיָן גָּמוּר וַאֲפִלּוּ חֲלִיצָה נַמִּי לֹא תִּבָּעֵי, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דִּצְרִיכָה חֲלִיצָה:


נכרית חולצת ולא מתיבמת – the same law applies even if he did not make a statement of intention [of levirate marriage], and he died, the non-related woman undergoes Halitzah, but she does not perform levirate marriage because she is the rival/co-wife of a married woman through levirate connection, and this that taught that one makes a statement of intention was in order to exclude this from the words of the School of Shammai who said that the statement of intention acquires a complete acquisition, and even Halitzah also is not requirement, which comes to teach us that she needs Halitzah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:7:1)


**שָׁעָה אֶחָת.** בִּנְפִילָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כְּשֶׁנָּפְלָה מֵאָחִיו הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁאָז הָיְתָה אִשְׁתּוֹ קַיֶּמֶת וְהָיְתָה זֹאת אֲסוּרָה עָלָיו כְּאֵשֶׁת אָח שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בָּנִים. וְצָרָתָהּ הַנָּכְרִית לֹא אִתְפָּרֵשׁ דִּינָהּ, וּמִסְתַּבְּרָא דְּחוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת:


שעה אחת – through the falling of the first [wife] when she fell from his first brother [who had died] and for since his wife is alive, and this one is prohibited to him as the wife of his brother for he has children, but, regarding her non-related rival/co-wife, the law is not explained and seems logical that she performs Halitzah but does not engage in levirate marriage.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:1)


**וְכֻלָּן.** חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה עֲרָיוֹת, שֶׁהָיוּ בָּהֶן לְאָחִיו קִדּוּשֵׁי סָפֵק אוֹ גֵּרוּשֵׁי סָפֵק, דְּהַוְיָא סְפֵק צָרַת עֶרְוָה:


וכולן – the fifteen [cases] of incest (see Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1), of a woman forbidden to a man on account of consanguinity that they had with his brothers a doubtful betrothal/Kiddushin or doubtful Jewish divorce for there is a doubt of the rivals of a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:2)


**חוֹלֶצֶת.** וְלֹא מִפְטְרָה בְּלֹא כְּלוּם, דְּדִלְמָא לָאו צָרַת עֶרְוָה הִיא:


חולצת – but it does not free her without anything for perhaps she is not the rival of a a woman forbidden on account of consanguinity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:8:3)


**סָפֵק קָרוֹב לוֹ.** שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁמֹנֶה אַמּוֹת מְצֻמְצָמוֹת בֵּינֵיהֶן בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת שֶׁל אָדָם קוֹנוֹת לוֹ שָׁם, וּזְרָקוֹ סָפֵק בְּאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹתָיו סָפֵק בְּאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹתֶיהָ:


ספק קרוב לו – for there were eight exactly defined cubits between them in the public domain, and the four cubits of a person acquires for him there, and he threw it, doubtfully within his four cubits and doubtfully within her four cubits.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:1)


**זִקַּת שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין.** דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא כְּנָסָהּ זֶה עֲדַיִן זִקַּת רִאשׁוֹן עָלָיו, וְנִתּוֹסְפָה עָלָיו זִקַּת שֵׁנִי בִּשְׁבִיל הַמַּאֲמָר, וּכְשֶׁמֵּת נוֹתְרוּ עָלֶיהָ זִקַּת שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין:


זיקת שני יבמין – for as long as this living brother didn’t marry her, the levirate relation of first is upon him and the levirate relation of the second was added upon him because of the statement of intention he made [to perform a levirate marriage], and when he died, there remained upon her the levirate relation of two brothers-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:2)
