**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** דְּקָסָבַר מְסַפְּקָא לָן אִי מַאֲמָר קוֹנֶה לְגַמְרֵי אוֹ לֹא קָנֵי כְּלָל, לְכָךְ מְיַבֵּם לְאֵיזוֹ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, דְּאִי מַאֲמָר קָנֵי לֹא הֲוֵי עֲלָהּ אֶלָּא זִקַּת שֵׁנִי, וְאִי מַאֲמָר לֹא קָנִי לֹא הֲוֵי עֲלָהּ אֶלָּא זִקַּת הָרִאשׁוֹן:


רש"א וכו' – since he holds that it is doubtful to us if the statement of intention [of performing levirate marriage] acquires completely or does not acquire at all, therefore, he performs levirate marriage on whichever of them that he desires, for if the statement of intention [to perform levirate marriage] acquires, he does not have anything other than the levirate relation of the second but if the statement of intention [of levirate marriage] does not acquire, he has nothing upon him other than the levirate relation of the first woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:9:3)


**וְחוֹלֵץ לַשְּׁנִיָּה.** דַּחֲדָא לֹא מְפַטְּרָהּ בְּבִיאַת אִידָךְ, דְּדִלְמָא מַאֲמָר לֹא קָנֵי וַהֲווֹ שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מִשְּׁנֵי בָּתִּים. וְיִבּוּמֵי תַּרְוַיְהוּ לֹא, דְּדִלְמָא מַאֲמָר קָנֵי וַהֲווֹ שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מִבַּיִת אֶחָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין דְּזִיקַת שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין דְּאוֹרַיְתָא מִקְּרָא דִּיבָמָהּ יָבֹא עָלֶיהָ, בַּגְּמָרָא מוּכָח דְּזִיקַת שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יֹאמְרוּ שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מִבַּיִת אֶחָד מִתְיַבְּמוֹת:


וחולץ לשניה – for one does not free him from [sexual intercourse] with the other, for perhaps the statement of intention [of levirate marriage] does not acquire, and there was two sisters-in-law that come from two homes. But there is no levirate marriage with both for perhaps the statement of intention [of levirate marriage] acquires, and there would be two sisters-in-law coming from one house. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon, and even though we state in our Mishnah that the levirate relation of the two brothers-in-law is from the Torah from a Biblical verse (Deuteronomy 25:5): “Her brother’s husband shall unite with her: [he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty],” in the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 31b) it proves hat the levirate relation of two brothers-in-law is Rabbinic as a decree, lest the people state that two sisters-in-law coming from the same house (i.e., widows of the same brother) may both be taken in levirate marriage.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:1)


**הָיוּ אַחִין.** אַף מִשּׁוּם אֵשֶׁת אָח וְכוּ'. אַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן אֵין אִסּוּר חָל עַל אִסּוּר, אִית לֵיהּ לְהַאי תַּנָּא דִּבְאִסּוּר כּוֹלֵל וְאִסּוּר מוֹסִיף וְאִסּוּר בַּת אַחַת כִּי הָכָא, אִסּוּר חָל עַל אִסּוּר, וְחַיָּב לְהָבִיא קָרְבָּן עַל כָּל אִסּוּר וְאִסּוּר:


היו אחין – even because of [the prohibition of] marrying the wife of your brother, etc., and even though we hold that this prohibition does not take effect upon another prohibition, this Tanna/teacher holds that an inclusive prohibition and a prohibition that adds and a simultaneous prohibition, like here, that a prohibition occurs on top of another prohibition and he is liable to bring a sacrifice on each and every prohibited act [performed] (i.e., a person may sometimes consume one piece of food and incur the penalty of four sin-offerings and one guilt offering - see Talmud Yevamot 34a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:2)


**וּמַפְרִישִׁין אוֹתָן.** שֶׁלֹּא לַחֲזֹר לְבַעֲלֵיהֶן:


ומפרישין אותן – so that they will not return to their husbands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:3)


**שֶׁמָּא מְעֻבָּרוֹת הֵן.** וְהַוְּלָדוֹת מַמְזֵרִים, וּבָעִינַן הַבְחָנָה בֵּין זֶרַע כָּשֵׁר לְזֶרַע פָּסוּל, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְלוּ הָעֻבָּרִים בְּבַעֲלֵיהֶן:


שמא מעוברות הן – and the offspring will be illegitimate/Mamzerim and we need a probe to distinguish between kosher seed/offspring and disqualified seed/offspring (i.e., seed that was sewn in holiness and seed that was sown in unholiness) so that the fetuses will not be left in doubt with [regard to] their husbands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:4)


**וְאִם הָיוּ כֹהֲנוֹת.** בְּנוֹת כֹּהֲנִים:


ואם היו כהנות – daughters of Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 3:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/3:10:5)


**נִפְסְלוּ מִתְּרוּמָה.** מִלֶּאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמַת בֵּית אָבִיהָ אֲפִלּוּ אַחַר מִיתַת בַּעֲלָהּ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה:


נפסלו מתרומה – from eating heave-offering (i.e., Priest’s due) of the house of her father, even after the death of her husband, and even though she was a ravaged woman.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:1:1)


**הַחוֹלֵץ לִיבִמְתּוֹ.** הוּא מֻתָּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ. דְּלָאו חֲלִיצָה הִיא, דְּהָא לֹא בַּעְיָא חֲלִיצָה. וּקְרוֹבוֹת וּקְרוֹבִים דְּמַתְנִיתִין הַיְנוּ הָנָךְ דְּנֶאֱסָרוֹת עַל הָאִישׁ מֵחֲמַת אִשְׁתּוֹ וּקְרוֹבִים הַנֶּאֱסָרִים עַל אִשָּׁה מֵחֲמַת בַּעֲלָהּ:


החולץ ליבמתו. הוא מותר בקרובותיה – for it is not Halitzah, for surely, she does not require Halitzah and the female and male relatives of our Mishnah, that is that those who are prohibited upon the man on account of his wife, and the male relatives who are prohibited to the wife on account of her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:1:2)


**אִם אֵין הַוָּלָד שֶׁל קְיָמָא.** שֶׁהוּא נֵפֶל:


אם אין הולד של קיימא – that he is premature/non-viable birth.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:2:1)


**וְחַיָּבִין בַּקָּרְבָּן.** שֶׁבָּעַל אֵשֶׁת אָח שֶׁלֹּא בִּמְקוֹם מִצְוָה. וְהַוָּלָד כָּשֵׁר מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ:


וחייבין בקרבן – for he had intercourse with his brother’s wife not in the place of the Mitzvah, and the offspring is kosher whichever way you turn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:2:2)


**וְחַיָּבִין אָשָׁם תָּלוּי.** שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר שֶׁחַיָּבִין עַל זְדוֹנוֹ כָּרֵת וְעַל שִׁגְגָתוֹ חַטָּאת, חַיָּבִין עַל לֹא הוֹדַע שֶׁלּוֹ אָשָׁם תָּלוּי:


וחייבין אשם תלוי – for everything where people are liable for extirpation for something done willfully, and for something done inadvertently for sin-offering, ae liable for something whether he became conscious [of his transgression] or not, a guilt offering is made when one is in doubt as to the commission of a sinful act (i.e., suspensive guilt-offering).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:1)


**שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם.** שֶׁהִיא מַמְתֶּנֶת וּמְצַפָּה לְיָבָם:


שומרת יבם = she is waiting and expecting to undergo levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:2)


**שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ נְכָסִים.** מִבֵּית אָבִיהָ:


נפלו לה נכסים – from her father’s house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:3)


**מַה יַּעֲשׂוּ בִכְתֻבָּתָהּ.** מָנֶה וּמָאתַיִם וְתוֹסֶפֶת וּנְדוּנְיָא שֶׁהִכְנִיסָה לוֹ וְקִבֵּל עָלָיו אַחֲרָיוּת:


מה יעשו בכתובתה – the [100] Maneh or the two-hundred and the supplement and the dowry that she brought in to him and he accepted responsibility for the surety (i.e., mortgaged property).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:4)


**וּבַנְּכָסִים הַנִּכְנָסִים וְיוֹצְאִים.** נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהִיא נִכְנֶסֶת נִכְנָסִין עִמָּהּ וּכְשֶׁיּוֹצֵאת מִבַּעֲלָהּ יוֹצְאִין עִמָּהּ:


ובנכסים היוצאין ונכנסין – the wife’s estate of which the husband has the fruition/usufruct without responsibility for loss or deterioration when she enters [into the marriage], they enter with her, and when she leaves from her husband, they go out with her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:5)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יַחֲלֹקוּ יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל עִם יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאָב.** דְּסָפֵק נְשׂוּאָה הִיא, וְזוֹכֶה הַיָּבָם בַּחֲצִי יְרֻשָּׁתָהּ מִסָּפֵק, שֶׁהַבַּעַל יוֹרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְזֶה שֶׁסָּפֵק נְשׂוּאָה הִיא אֶצְלוֹ זוֹכֶה בְפַלְגָּא:


בש"א יחלוקו יורשי הבעל עם יורשי האב – for she was doubtfully married and the levir takes possession of one-half of her inheritance from doubt for the husband inherits his wife but this case where she is doubtfully married to him, he takes possession of one-half.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:3:6)


**נְכָסִים בְּחֶזְקָתָן.** נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל, בְּחֶזְקָתָן. וְלֹא פֵּרְשׁוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהָיוּ הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁלָּהּ, אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל שֶׁאַחֲרָיוּתָם עָלָיו. וּלְעִנְיַן דִּינָא יַחֲלֹקוּ אֲפִלּוּ לְדִבְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל:


נכסים בחזקתן – mort-main (i.e., the wife’s estate held by her husband, which, in case of her death or divorce, he must restore in specie, being responsible with all his landed property for loss or deterioration (see Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 1) in their presumptive legal status, but the School of Hillel did not explain whether the properties were in the possession of the inheritors of the woman or whether her properties were in the possession of the inheritors of the husband, for their surety is upon him, but as regards to law, they are divided, even according to the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:4:1)


**כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ לְכָל דָּבָר.** שֶׁמְּגָרְשָׁהּ בְּגֵט וְלֹא בַּעְיָא חֲלִיצָה, וּמַחְזִירָהּ כְּשֶׁיִּרְצֶה וְלֹא מִתְּסְרָא עֲלֵיהּ:


כאשתו לכל דבר – that he divorces her with a Jewish bill of divorce and she does not require Halitzah and he restores her when he desires, and is not forbidden to her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:4:2)


**עַל נִכְסֵי בַעְלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ קַרְקָעוֹת שֶׁל בַּעֲלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן אַחֲרָאִין וְעַרְבָאִין עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. וְאִי לֵית לָהּ כְּתֻבָּה מִבַּעֲלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן כּוֹתֵב לָהּ הַיָּבָם כְּתֻבָּה עַל נְכָסָיו, וְהִיא מָנֶה בִּלְבַד:


על נכסי בעלה הראשון – that the landed property of her first husband will be responsible and vouched on her Ketubah, and if she lacks a Ketubah from her first husband, the levir writes her a Ketubah on his property which is only a Maneh.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:5:1)


**מִצְוָה בַגָּדוֹל לְיַבֵּם.** כִּדְיָלְפִינַן מִוְּהָיָה הַבְּכוֹר:


מצוה בגדול ליבם – as we derive from (Deuteronomy 25:6): “The first son [that she bears shall be accounted to the dead brother].”



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:1)


**וּבַגָּדוֹל.** שֶׁהָיָה גְּדוֹל הָאַחִים בִּמְדִינַת הַיָּם, וְתָלָה גְּדוֹל הָאַחִין שֶׁלְּפָנֵינוּ בּוֹ לוֹמַר הַמְתִּינוּ לוֹ, שֶׁהוּא גָּדוֹל מִמֶּנִּי:


ובגדול – for the oldest of the brothers was abroad and he assigns the oldest of the brothers that is before us to state that we should wait for him, for he is older than me.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:2)


**וּבַחֵרֵשׁ וּבַשּׁוֹטֶה.** עַד שֶׁיַּבְרִיאוּ. אֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לוֹ:


בחרש ובשוטה] – until they recover, we don’t listen to him].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:6:3)


**עָלֶיךָ מִצְוָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָחִיךָ שֶׁבִּמְדִינַת הַיָּם הוּא גָּדוֹל מִמְּךָ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאַתָּה גְּדוֹל הָאַחִין שֶׁלְּפָנֵינוּ עָלֶיךָ מִצְוָה, דְּשִׁהוּיֵי מִצְוָה לֹא מַשְׁהִינַן:


עליך מצוה – even though your brother is abroad, he is older than you. Since you ae the oldest of the brothers in our presence, upon you is the Mitzvah [of levirate marriage] for the delay in the performance of the commandment, we do not delay.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:1)


**הֲרֵי הוּא כְאֶחָד מִן הָאַחִין.** וְלֹא הִפְסִיד חֶלְקוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁחָלַץ. דְּסָלְקָא דַּעְתָּךְ אֲמִינָא לִקְנְסֵיהּ הוֹאִיל וְהִפְסִידָהּ מִיִּבּוּם, שֶׁפְּסָלָהּ עַל הָאַחִים:


הרי הוא כחד מן האחין – and he did not lose his portion because he performed Halitzah for you might think that I would say that he would be fined for since he caused her to lose from [having] levirate marriage for she was disqualified on [his] brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:2)


**זָכָה בְּנִכְסֵי אָחִיו.** וַאֲפִלּוּ גֵּרְשָׁהּ לְמָחָר, דְּיָקוּם עַל שֵׁם אָחִיו אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא, וַהֲרֵי קָם:


זכה בנכסי אחיו – and even if he would divorced her on the morrow, for the All-Merciful one stated (Deuteronomy 25:6): “[The first son that she bears] shall be accounted to the [dead] brother,” and he has stood up [and performed his duty].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:3)


**נְכָסִים שֶׁל אָב.** דִּכְתִיב וְהָיָה הַבְּכוֹר, מַה בְּכוֹר אֵין לוֹ בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב, אַף יָבָם אֵין לוֹ בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב. וְאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא הַכּוֹנֵס אֶת יְבִמְתּוֹ זָכָה בְּנִכְסֵי אָחִיו וַאֲפִלּוּ בִּמְקוֹם אָב:


נכסים של אב – as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:6): “The first son [that she bears shall be accounted to the dead brother],” just he doesn’t have a first-born son during the life of the father, even the levir doesn’t have it during the lifetime of the father, and such is the Halakha but rather, a man who marries his sister-in-law takes possession of the property of his brother, and even in the place of the father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:4)


**אָסוּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ.** כְּאִלּוּ הִיא אִשְׁתּוֹ, וְכָל קְרוֹבוֹת הַנֶּאֱסָרוֹת מֵחֲמַת אִשָּׁה גְּמוּרָה אֲסוּרוֹת מִדְּרַבָּנָן בַּחֲלוּצָה:


אסור בקרובותיה – as if she is his wife and all of the female relations that are forbidden [to him] on account of the woman is completely his wife, are forbidden for Halitzah according to the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:5)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא קַיֶּמֶת.** אַאֲחוֹתָהּ קָאֵי וְלֹא אַשְּׁאָר עֲרָיוֹת:


בזמן שהיא קיימת – it refers to her sister, and not to the rest of the women forbidden on account of consanguinity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:6)


**מֻתָּר אָדָם בִּקְרוֹבַת צָרַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן צָרָה כַּחֲלוּצָה שֶׁיְּהֵא אָסוּר בַּאֲחוֹת צָרַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אָסוּר בַּאֲחוֹת חֲלוּצָתוֹ:


מותר אדם בקרובת צרת חלוצתו – and we don’t say that the rival/co-wife is like the woman who has undergone Halitzah, that he would be forbidden with the sister of the rival/co-wife of the woman with whom he performed Halitzah, like he is prohibited [to be] with the sister of the woman with whom he performed Halitzah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:7:7)


**וְאָסוּר בְּצָרַת קְרוֹבַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ.** רְאוּבֵן חָלַץ לְלֵאָה, וְרָחֵל נְשׂוּאָה לְנָכְרִי וְלָהּ צָרָה, וּמֵת הַנָּכְרִי, אֲסוּרָה אוֹתָהּ צָרָה לִרְאוּבֵן. וְטַעְמָא הֲוֵי, שֶׁבִּזְמַן שֶׁלֵּאָה חוֹלֶצֶת, מוֹלִיכָה רָחֵל אֲחוֹתָהּ עִמָּהּ לְבֵית דִּין, וְכֻלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא יָדְעֵי לְהֵי מִנַּיהוּ חָלִיץ, וְאִיכָּא דְּסָבְרֵי דִּלְרָחֵל חָלִיץ, וְכִי נָסִיב צָרַת רָחֵל דְּהַיְנוּ צָרַת קְרוֹבַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ אָמְרֵי צָרַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ נָסִיב, דְּאִינְהוּ סָבְרֵי שֶׁרָחֵל וְצָרָתָהּ נְשֵׁי אָחִיו הָיוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי חָלַץ לְרָחֵל. אֲבָל אֵין הַיְבָמָה מוֹלִיכָה צָרָתָהּ עִמָּהּ בִּזְמַן שֶׁחוֹלֶצֶת, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי נָסִיב אֲחוֹת הַצָּרָה לֹא אָמְרֵי אֲחוֹת חֲלוּצָה נָסִיב:


ואסור בצרת קרובת חלוצתו – Reuven who performed Halitzah with Leah and Rachel is married to a foreign (i.e. unrelated Jewish) man, and she has a rival/co-wife, and the foreign (i.e., unrelated Jewish) man died, that rival/co-wife is prohibited to Reuven. And the reason for this is at the time when Leah had the Halitzah ceremony performed, she brought Rachel her sister with her to the Jewish court, and the entire world doesn’t know to which of them had Halitzah and there are those who think that to Rachel was peformed Halitzah, and when the rival/co-wife of Rachel gets married, that is the rival/co-wife of the the relative of his Halitzah, they say that the rival/co-wife of his Halitzah married, and they think that Rachl and her rival/co-wife were the wives of his brother, because he did Halitzah to Rachel. But, the sister-in-law does not bring her rival/co-wife with her at the time that she has the Halitzah ceremony performed, therefore, when the sister of her rival/co-wife gets married, we don’t say that it the sister of the woman who had Halitzah that married.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:8:1)


**וְכֵן הַמְגָרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וְכוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, וְכֵן סָלְקָא דַּעְתָּךְ. אֶלָּא אֵימָא אֲבָל הַמְגָרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וְכוּ', שֶׁאֲחוֹת גְּרוּשָׁה מִדִּבְרֵי תּוֹרָה וּלְכָךְ פְּטוּרָה מִן הַחֲלִיצָה וּמִן הַיִּבּוּם, וַאֲחוֹת חֲלוּצָה מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים וּלְכָךְ חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת:


וכן המגרש את אשתו וכו' – In the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 41a), it raises the question and similarly, you might think, but rather, I would say, that a man who divorces his wife, etc., that the sister of the divorced woman from the words of the Torah, and therefore is exempt from Halitzah and from levirate marriage, but the sister of the woman who underwent Halitzah, according to the words of the Scribes/Soferim, so therefore, she engages in Halitzah does not engage in levirate marriage.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:1)


**שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם.** מַמְתֶּנֶת לַיָּבָם:


שומרת יבם – she is waiting to perform levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:2)


**שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ.** אֶחָד מִן הָאַחִים אֶת אֲחוֹתָהּ אַחַר שֶׁנָּפְלָה זוֹ לִפְנֵיהֶן וְהֻזְקְקָה לְכֻלָּן:


שקדש – one of the brothers [who betrothed] her sister after this one fell before them and she became dependent upon all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:3)


**הַמְתֵּן.** מִלִּכְנֹס, דְּקָא פָּגַעְתָּ בַּאֲחוֹת זְקוּקָה:


המתן – from marrying for she made contact [with the sister of] her levirate connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:4)


**עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה אָחִיךָ מַעֲשֶׂה.** שֶׁיִּכְנֹס אָחִיךָ אֶת הַיְבָמָה אוֹ יַחְלֹץ לָהּ וְיַעֲקֹר אֶת זִקָּתָהּ מֵעָלֶיךָ:


עד שישה אחיך מעשה – that your brother will marry the sister-in-law or perform Halitzah to her and uproot her levirate relation from you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:5)


**מֵתָה הַיְּבָמָה יִכְנֹס אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ כְּנָסָהּ וּמֵתָה מֻתָּר בַּאֲחוֹתָהּ:


מתה היבמה יכנוס את אשתו – for even if he married her and she died, he is permitted to her sister.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:6)


**מֵת הַיָּבָם.** וְלֹא נוֹתַר אָח אֶלָּא זֶה שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ אֶת אֲחוֹתָהּ:


מת היבם – and no brother remains other than this one who betrothed her sister.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:9:7)


**מוֹצִיא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ בְגֵט וְאֶת אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו בַּחֲלִיצָה.** אֲבָל יִבּוּמֵי לֹא, מִשּׁוּם דַּאֲחוֹת גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ הִיא. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּסִיק הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶּן בְּתֵירָא:


מוציא את אשתו בגט ואת אשת אחיו בחליצה – but not perform levirate marriage for she is the sister of his divorced wife, and in the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 41a), the legal decision is according to Rabbi Yehuda ben Beteyra.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:1)


**לֹא תַחֲלֹץ וְכוּ'.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא טַעֲמָא, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) וְאִם לֹא יַחְפֹּץ הָאִישׁ לָקַחַת אֶת יְבִמְתּוֹ וְעָלְתָה יְבִמְתּוֹ וְגוֹ', הָא אִם יַחְפֹּץ יְיַבֵּם, כָּל הָעוֹלֶה לְיִבּוּם עוֹלֶה לַחֲלִיצָה וְכָל שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹלֶה לְיִבּוּם אֵינוֹ עוֹלֶה לַחֲלִיצָה:


לא תחלוץ וכו' - In the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 41b), it explains the reason, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:7): “But if the man does not want to marry his brother’s widow, his brother’s widow shall appear [before the elders in the gate and declare, ‘My husband’s brother refuses to establish a name in Israel for his brother; he will not perform the duty of the levir’],” but if he desires, he should perform the duty of the levir (i.e., perform levirate marriage). All who go up for levirate marriage go up for Halitzah and all who do not go up for levirate marriage do not go up for Halitzah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:2)


**בְּתוּלוֹת.** גָּזְרִינַן אַטּוּ בְּעוּלוֹת, וַאֲרוּסוֹת אַטּוּ נְשׂוּאוֹת, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה. וְהָנָךְ שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים הֵם תִּשְׁעִים יוֹם חוּץ מִיּוֹם שֶׁמֵּת בּוֹ בַּעֲלָהּ אוֹ שֶׁנִּתְגָּרְשָׁה בּוֹ וְחוּץ מִיּוֹם שֶׁנִּתְאָרְסָה בּוֹ:


בתולות – we make the decree because of women who have had sexual relations, and betrothed woman because of married women, and such is the Halakha. And these three months are ninety days excluding from it the day her husband died, or when she was divorced from him, and excluding from it the day when she became betrothed to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:3)


**הַנְּשׂוּאוֹת יִתְאָרְסוּ.** דְּטַעֲמָא דַּאֲסוּרָה כְּדֵי לְהַבְחִין בֵּין זֶרַע רִאשׁוֹן לְזֶרַע שֵׁנִי, שֶׁלֹּא תֵלֵד בֵּן סָפֵק בֶּן תִּשְׁעָה לָרִאשׁוֹן סָפֵק בֶּן שִׁבְעָה לָאַחֲרוֹן, וּבָאֲרוּסוֹת לֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר הָכִי:


הנשואות יתארסו – that the reason that she is forbidden in order to distinguish between the seed of the first to the seed of the second, so that she should not give birth to a undetermined/doubtful son (i.e., so that we would know who the actual father was), a son of seven months [pregnancy] to the first [husband] or doubtfully a son of seven [months] to the latter [husband] and with regard to betrothed women, one doesn’t not have to say this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:4)


**וְהָאֲרוּסוֹת יִנָּשְׂאוּ.** דְּהָא לֹא אִיעַבּוּר מֵרִאשׁוֹן:


והארוסות ינשאו – for she was not made pregnant from the first [husband].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:5)


**חוּץ מֵאֲרוּסָה שֶׁבִּיהוּדָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְיַחֲדִין אֶת הַכַּלָּה וְאֶת הֶחָתָן בִּיהוּדָה קֹדֶם כְּנִיסָתָן לַחֻפָּה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה רָגִיל וּמְשַׂחֵק עִמָּהּ וְלֹא יִהְיוּ בֹּשִׁים זֶה מִזֶּה בִּבְעִילַת מִצְוָה, לְכָךְ חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא בָּעַל:


חוץ מארוסה שביהודה – for they would bring together the bride and the groom in Judea prior to their entrance to the wedding canopy in order that he would be used to her and he would sport with her and they would not be embarrassed one from the other through the commanded act of sexual intimacy; therefore, we suspect that perhaps he had sexual intercourse [with her].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:10:6)


**כָּל הַנָּשִׁים יִתְאָרְסוּ.** מִשּׁוּם דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה לֹא מַפְלִיג בֵּין אַלְמָנָה לִגְרוּשָׁה וּמַתִּיר לָאַלְמָנָה לֵאָרֵס אֲפִלּוּ תּוֹךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם שֶׁל יְמֵי אֶבְלָהּ, אָתָא רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לְאִפְלוּגֵי עֲלֵיהּ בְּהָא, וְאָסַר לָאַלְמָנָה לֵאָרֵס תּוֹךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים שֶׁל יְמֵי אֶבְלָהּ:


כל הנשים יתארסו – because Rabbi Yehuda does not distinguish between widows to divorcees and permits for the widow to be betrothed even within thrirty days of the days of her mourning; Rabbi Yosi comes to dispute him on this and prohibits the widow to become betrothed within thirty days of her days of mourning.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:1)


**אַרְבָּעָה אַחִים וְכוּ'.** הוּא הַדִּין אֲפִלּוּ נָשֵׁי טוּבָא נַמִּי, אִם אֶפְשָׁר בְּסִפּוּקַיְהוּ, הָרְשׁוּת בְּיָדוֹ לִכְנֹס אֶת כֻּלָּן. וְהַאי דְּנָקַט אַרְבַּע, עֵצָה טוֹבָה קָמַשְׁמַע לָן, אַרְבַּע אִין טְפֵי לֹא, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּנִמְטַיְהוּ עוֹנָה בְּחֹדֶשׁ:


ארבעה אחים וכו' – and the same law applies even also with many wives, if it is possible to support them, permission is in his bands to marry all of them, and the fact that it (i.e., the Mishnah) took four is good advice: for it comes to teach us, for yes, more than that, no, for just as the duty of marital visits at certain intervals arrives during the month.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:2)


**בִּיאָתָהּ אוֹ חֲלִיצָתָהּ שֶׁל אַחַת מֵהֶן פּוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ.** וְלֹא מְיַבֵּם לְתַרְוַיְהוּ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִבְנֶה אֶת בֵּית אָחִיו, בַּיִת אֶחָד הוּא בּוֹנֶה וְאֵינוֹ בּוֹנֶה שְׁנֵי בָּתִּים. וְלֹא חָלִיץ לְתַרְוַיְהוּ, שֶׁכָּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹלֶה לְיִבּוּם אֵינוֹ עוֹלֶה לַחֲלִיצָה, וְהוֹאִיל וְתַרְוַיְהוּ כִּי הֲדָדֵי לֹא סָלְקֵי לְיִבּוּם כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בַּיִת אֶחָד הוּא בּוֹנֶה, הָכִי נַמִּי לֹא סָלְקֵי לַחֲלִיצָה:


ביאתה או חליצתה פוטרת צרתה – and he doesn’t perform levirate marriage with both of them, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:9): “who will not build up his brother’s house.” One house he builds, but he does not build up two houses. And he doesn’t perform Halitzah with both of them, and whomever does not come up for levirate marrae does not come up for Halitzah, for since that both of them like each other do not go up for lievirate marriage, as we stated, he builds one house,” so also, they don’t go up for Halitzah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:11:3)


**חוֹלֵץ לַפְּסוּלָה.** וְלֹא יִפְסֹל אֶת הַכְּשֵׁרָה לִכְהֻנָּה, שֶׁהַחֲלוּצָה פְּסוּלָה לִכְהֻנָּה, וְלֹא יִשְׁפֹּךְ אָדָם מֵי בּוֹרוֹ וַאֲחֵרִים צְרִיכִין לָהֶן:


חולץ לפסולה – and he will not disqualify that [woman who is] fit [to marry into] the priesthood, for a woman who has undergone Halitzah is disqualified from the priesthood, and a man should not empty the waters of his cistern and others need it.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:1)


**הַמַּחְזִיר אֶת גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ.** מִשֶּׁנִּשֵּׂאת:


מהחזיר את גרושתו – from whom he was married.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:2)


**וְהַנּוֹשֵׂא אֶת חֲלוּצָתוֹ.** דְּקַיְמָא עֲלֵיהּ בְּלָאו דְּלֹא יִבְנֶה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא בָּנָה שׁוּב לֹא יִבְנֶה:


והנושא את חלוצתו – since we establish regarding him with the negative commandment (Deuteronomy 25:9): “ [Thus shall be done to the man] who will not build up his brother’s house,” since he did not “build up,” he furthermore will not build up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:3)


**וְהַנּוֹשֵׂא קְרוֹבַת חֲלוּצָתוֹ.** דְּקָסָבַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דַּחֲלוּצָתוֹ כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ, שֶׁהַכָּתוּב קְרָאָהּ בֵּיתוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (שם) בֵּית חֲלוּץ הַנָּעַל. וְקָסָבַר נַמִּי רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא יֵשׁ מַמְזֵר מֵחַיָּבֵי לָאוִין. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה:


והנושא קרובת חלוצתו – since Rabbi Akiva holds that the woman who takes off the shoe and spits in his face (by refusing to perform levirate marriage) is like his wife, and Scripture calls her, “his house,” as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:10): “the family of the un-sandaled one.” And also, Rabbi Akiva holds that there is a Mamzer from those who are liable for violating negative commandments, but this is not the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:12:4)


**קְרוֹבַת גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ.** דִּגְרוּשָׁתוֹ כְּאִשְׁתּוֹ. וְאִמָּהּ וַאֲחוֹתָהּ חַיָּבֵי כְּרִיתוּת נִינְהוּ:


קרובת גרושתו – for his divorced wife is like his wife, and her mother and her sister [if they are subsequently married to him] make him liable for extirpation.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:13:1)


**כָּל שְׁאֵר בָּשָׂר שֶׁהוּא בְלֹא יָבֹא.** כָּל קִרְבָה שֶׁהִיא בְּלָאו, וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵין בָּהּ כָּרֵת:


כל שאר בשר שהוא בלא יבא – every nearness which is [a violation of] a negative commandment and even if there is no extirpation [attached to it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/4:13:2)


**כָּל חַיָּבֵי כָּרֵת.** אֲבָל לֹא חַיָּבֵי לָאוִין. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה, שֶׁכָּל שֶׁאִסּוּרוֹ אִסּוּר כָּרֵת הַוָּלָד מַמְזֵר, חוּץ מִן הַנִּדָּה. אֲבָל מֵחַיָּבֵי לָאוִין אֵין הַוָּלָד מַמְזֵר:


כל חייבי כרת – but not those who are liable [for violating] a negative commandment, and such is the Halakha. For all that whose prohibition is a prohibition of extirpation, the offspring is illegitimate/Mamzer, except from that of a menstruant woman, but with those who violate negative commandments, the offspring are not Mamzerim/illegitimate.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:1)


**רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל.** אֵין גֵּט אַחַר גֵּט. שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת לְיָבָם אֶחָד מֵאָח אֶחָד, וְנָתַן גֵּט לָרִאשׁוֹנָה וְחָזַר וְנָתַן גֵּט לַשְּׁנִיָּה, לֹא נֶאֶסְרוּ קְרוֹבוֹת הַשְּׁנִיָּה עָלָיו לְמֵהֲוֵי כִּגְרוּשָׁתוֹ, דִּמְכִי יָהֵיב גֵּט לָרִאשׁוֹנָה פָּקְעָה זִקַּת שְׁתֵּיהֶן מֵעָלָיו, דְּגֵט מַהֲנֵי בִּיבָמָה לְמֵיקַם עֲלֵהּ בְּלֹא יִבְנֶה, וְשׁוּב לֹא יְיַבֵּם לֹא הוּא וְלֹא שְׁאָר הָאַחִים לֹא לַיְּבָמָה שֶׁקִּבְּלָה הַגֵּט וְלֹא לְצָרָתָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי הֲדַר וְיָהִיב גֵּט שֵׁנִי לַשְּׁנִיָּה אֵין בְּמַעֲשָׂיו כְּלוּם וַהֲוֵי כְּנוֹתֵן גֵּט לְאִשָּׁה נָכְרִית דְּעָלְמָא. וְכֵן שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין לִיבָמָה אַחַת וְנָתַן זֶה גֵּט וְזֶה גֵּט, הָאַחֲרוֹן אֵינוֹ כְּלוּם וּמֻתָּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ:


רבן גמליאל. אין גט אחר גט – two widows without children from a deceased husband awaiting a levirate connection of one levir from one brother, and he gave a Jewish bill of divorce to the first, and then returned and gave a Jewish bill of divorce to the second [woman], the female relatives of the second are not prohibited to him , for that would be like his divorced wife. For when he gives a Jewish bill of divorce to the first [woman], the levirate relationship has no legal effect on both of them from him for the Jewish bill of divorce is beneficial for the widow without children from a deceased husband to maintain her [with the Biblical verse] (Deuteronomy 25:9): “who will not build up [his brother’s house],” and furthermore, he will not perform levirate marriage, neither him nor his other brothers not for the Yevamah/widow whose husband died without issue nor her co-wife. Therefore, when he returns and gives a second Jewish bill of divorce to the second [widow], there is nothing in his actions, and it is like he is giving a Jewish bill of divorce to a mere stranger. And similarly, two levirs to one widow without children whose husband is deceased and this one [levir] gave a Jewish bill of divorce and that [levir] gave a Jewish bill of divorce, the latter is not anything and he is permitted to her female relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:2)


**וְלֹא מַאֲמָר אַחַר מַאֲמָר.** בֵּין שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין לִיבָמָה אַחַת בֵּין בְּיָבָם אֶחָד וּשְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת, הָאַחֲרוֹן אֵינוֹ כְּלוּם וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה מִמֶּנּוּ גֵּט וּמֻתָּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ:


ולא מאמר אחר מאמר -.between two levirs and one widow whose husband died without children, or with one levir and two widows. The latter is nothing and she doesn’t require from him a Jewish bill of divorce and he is permitted to her female relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:1:3)


**יֵשׁ גֵּט אַחַר גֵּט.** דְּקַמָּא לֹא דָּחָה זִקָּתָהּ לְגַמְרֵי, דְּהָא בַּעְיָא חֲלִיצָה, הִלְכָּךְ אַכַּתִּי אִיכָּא פַּלְגָא זִקָּה וְאַהֲנֵי גֵּט שֵׁנִי לַשְּׁנִיָּה לְמֵהֲוֵי גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ וְאָסוּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


יש גט אחר גט – the first [Jewish bill of divorce] did not supersede her levirate relationship completely for she requires Halitzah; therefore, there is still one-half of her levirate relationship and the second Jewish bill of divorce to the second [widow] is beneficial for his divorced wife and he is prohibited to her relatives, and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:1)


**כֵּיצַד.** לָאו אַפְּלֻגְתָּא קָאֵי אֶלָּא מִלְּתָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשָׁהּ, וְאַיָּבָם אֶחָד וִיבָמָה אַחַת קָאֵי. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, כֵּיצַד דִּין יָבָם אֶחָד וִיבָמָה אַחַת:


כיצד – this does not refer to the dispute, but rather, it is a matter of its own, referring to one levir and one widow whose husband died without children, and this is what he said: In what case is the law of one levir and one Yevamah/widow whose husband died without children?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:2)


**צְרִיכָה הֵימֶנּוּ חֲלִיצָה.** וְאִם רָצָה לִכְנֹס לֹא יִכְנֹס, דְּכֵיוָן דְּהִתְחִיל בְּגֵרוּשִׁין קַיְמָא עֲלֵיהּ בְּלֹא יִבְנֶה:


צריכה הימנו חליצה – if he wanted to consummate a marriage by conducting a woman to his house/to wed, he should not do so for since he had begun the Jewish divorce, it is fulfilled for him through [the Biblical verse] (Deuteronomy 25:9) “who will not build up [his brother’s house].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:2:3)


**צְרִיכָה הֵימֶנּוּ גֵּט.** דַּחֲלִיצָה אַפְקַעְתָּא לַזִּקָּה, וְגֵט בָּעֵי לְאַפְקוּעֵי קִדּוּשִׁין דִּילֵיהּ, דַּחֲלִיצָה לֹא מַפְקְעָא קִדּוּשִׁין:


צריכה ממנו גט – for Halitzah causes a release from her levirate relationship and the Jewish bill of divorce need to cancel his Kiddushin/betrothal for Halitzah has no legal effect on Kiddushin/betrothal.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:1)


**נָתַן גֵּט וְעָשָׂה בָהּ מַאֲמָר צְרִיכָה גֵט וַחֲלִיצָה.** דְּגֵט דָּחִי קְצָת וְשִׁיֵּר קְצָת, וּמַאֲמָר קָנִי שְׁיוּרָא דְּגֵט, הִלְכָּךְ צְרִיכָה גֵּט לְמַאֲמָרוֹ וַחֲלִיצָה לְזִקָּתוֹ:


נתן גט ועשה בה מאמר צריכה גט וחליצה – that the Jewish bill of divorce supersedes part [of the marriage] and retains part, and the statement [of intention of marriage by a levir] acquires the remnant of the Jewish bill of divorce, therefore, she requires a Jewish bill of divorce for his (i.e., the levir’s) statement [of intention] and Halitzah for the levirate connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:2)


**נָתַן גֵּט וּבָעַל.** אָסוּר לְקַיְּמָהּ, שֶׁמִּשָּׁעָה שֶׁנָּתַן גֵּט קַיְמָא עֲלֵיהּ בְּלֹא יִבְנֶה, וּצְרִיכָה גֵּט לְבִיאָתוֹ וַחֲלִיצָה לְזִקָּתוֹ:


נתן גט ובעל – it is prohibited to sustain her [in marriage] since from the time that he gave a Jewish bill of divorce , he fulfills, regarding her (Deuteronomy 25:9): “who will not build up [his brother’s house],” and she requires a Jeiwsh bill of divorce for his coition and Halitzah for the levirate connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:3)


**אֵין אַחַר חֲלִיצָה כְלוּם.** הָא מַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא הִיא דְּאָמַר אֵין קִדּוּשִׁין תּוֹפְסִין בְּחַיָּבֵי לָאוִין. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא הֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים, שֶׁאוֹמְרִים יֵשׁ אַחַר חֲלִיצָה כְלוּם, וְאִם קִדְּשָׁהּ אַחַר חֲלִיצָה צְרִיכָה הֵימֶנּוּ גֵּט:


אין לאחר חליצה כלום – This Mishnah is according to Rabbi Akiva, who stated, that Kiddushin/betrothal does not take effect with those who a liable for negative commandments, but it is not the Halakha. Rather, the Halakha is according to the Sages who state that there is nothing after Halitzah, and if he betrothed her after Halitzah, she requires from him a Jewish bill of divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:3:4)


**אַחַת יְבָמָה אַחַת לְיָבָם אֶחָד וְאַחַת שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת לְיָבָם אֶחָד.** שָׁוִין הֵן שֶׁיֵּשׁ אַחַר גֵּט רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ אַחַר מַאֲמָר רִאשׁוֹן כְּלוּם וְאֵין אַחַר בִּיאָה שֶׁבַּתְּחִלָּה כְּלוּם וְאֵין אַחַר חֲלִיצָה כְלוּם:


אחד יבמה אחת ליבם אחד ואחד שתי יבמות ליבם אחד – they are both equivalent for there is after the first Jewish bill of divorce or after the first statement [of intention of the Levir] nothing, but after the coition at the beginning, there is nothing, and there is nothing after Halitzah.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:1)


**צְרִיכוֹת שְׁנֵי גִטִּין.** לִשְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת. כְּרַבָּנָן דְּיֵשׁ מַאֲמָר אַחַר מַאֲמָר:


צריכות שני גיטין – for both Yevamot/widows whose husbands died without children according to the Rabbis for there is a statement [of intention of the levir] after a statement [of intention of the levir].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:2)


**וַחֲלִיצָה.** לְאַחַת מֵהֶן, וּפוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ:


וחליצה – to one of them and this exempts her rival/co-wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:3)


**מַאֲמָר לָזוֹ וְגֵט לָזוֹ.** פָּסַל גֵּט שֶׁל אַחֲרוֹנָה אֶת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, דְּקָם לֵיהּ בְּלֹא יִבְנֶה, וּצְרִיכָה גֵּט לְמַאֲמָרוֹ וַחֲלִיצָה לְזִקָּתוֹ:


מאמר לזו וגט ולזו – the Jewish bill of divorce to the latter [wife] invalidates that of the first [wife] he has been declared as one “who will not build up [his brother’s house]” (Deuteronomy 25:9 – see Talmud Yevamot 32a), and she requires a Jewish bill of divorce for his declaration [of intention for levirate marriage] and Halitzah for his levirate connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:4)


**מַאֲמָר לָזוֹ וּבָעַל לָזוֹ צְרִיכוֹת שְׁנֵי גִטִּין.** זוֹ גֵּט לְמַאֲמָרוֹ וְזוֹ גֵּט לְבִיאָתוֹ:


מאמר לזו ובעל לזו צריכות שני גיטין – for this one, a Jewish bill of divorce for his declaration [of intention for levirate marriage] and for the other [woman] a Jewish bill of divorce for his act of coition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:5)


**וַחֲלִיצָה.** לְאַחַת מֵהֶן וּפוֹטֶרֶת צָרָתָהּ:


וחליצה – to one of them and this exempts her rival/co-wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:6)


**גֵּט לָזוֹ וּבָעַל לָזוֹ צְרִיכָה גֵּט וַחֲלִיצָה.** וְאָסוּר לְקַיְּמָהּ, דְּקָם עֲלֵיהּ בְּלֹא יִבְנֶה מִשּׁוּם גֵּט רִאשׁוֹן, וּבְגֵט לְחוּדָא לֹא נַפְקָה בַּעֲלַת בִּיאָה, דְּבִיאָה פְּסוּלָה הִיא:


גט לזו ובעל לזו צריכות גט וחליצה – and it is prohibited to sustain her for he has been declared as one “who will not build up [his brother’s house]” (Deuteronomy 25:9), because of the first Jewish bill of divorce. But with a Jewish bill of divorce by itself, does not free woman who had a sexual act because it was a disqualifying act of coition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:4:7)


**אֵין לְאַחַר חֲלִיצָה כְּלוּם.** וְאִי הֲדַר מְקַדֵּשׁ לָהּ אוֹ לְצָרָתָהּ לֹא תָּפְסֵי בָהּ קִדּוּשִׁין, כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דְּאָמַר אֵין קִדּוּשִׁין תּוֹפְסִין בְּחַיָּבֵי לָאוִין. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה:


אין לאחר חליצה כלום – and if afterwards he betrothed her or her rival/co-wife, Kiddushin/betrothal does not take effect with her, as per Rabbi Akiva, who said that Kiddushin does not take effect in those who are liable for violation of negative commandments, but it is not the Halakha.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:5:1)


**חָלַץ וְחָלַץ.** מֻתָּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹת שְׁנִיָּה, שֶׁאֵין אַחַר חֲלִיצָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שׁוּם זִקַּת יְבָמִין, וַחֲלִיצָה בַּתְרַיְתָא לָאו כְּלוּם הִיא, וַאֲפִלּוּ לִכְהֻנָּה אֵינָהּ פְּסוּלָה. וְכֵן חָלַץ לְזוֹ וּמַאֲמָר לְזוֹ, וְכֵן נָתַן גֵּט לְזוֹ אַחַר הַחֲלִיצָה שֶׁל זוֹ, אוֹ אִם בָּעַל לְזוֹ אַחַר הַחֲלִיצָה שֶׁל זוֹ, אֵין מַעֲשֶׂה הָאַחֲרוֹן כְּלוּם. אוֹ אִם בָּעַל תְּחִלָּה וְחָזַר וּבָעַל לַשְּׁנִיָּה, אוֹ בָּעַל תְּחִלָּה וְעָשָׂה מַאֲמָר בַּשְּׁנִיָּה, אוֹ נָתַן גֵּט לַשְּׁנִיָּה אוֹ חָלַץ לַשְּׁנִיָּה, אֵין אַחַר בִּיאָה כְּלוּם, וּמַעֲשֶׂה אַחֲרוֹן לֹא חָשִׁיב וּמֻתָּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹת הַשְּׁנִיָּה:


חלץ וחלץ – he is permitted [to be married to] the relatives of the second [woman] for there is nothing after the first Halitzah any levirate connections, and Halitzah with both is nothing, for even to the priesthood, she is not invalidated. And similarly, if he performed Halitzah with the first [widow of the deceased childless brother] and made a statement [of intention of levirate marriage] with the second [widow], and similarly, if he had given a Jewish bill of divorce to this [second] one after performing Halitzah with that one, or performed an act of coition with this one after the Halitzah of that one, the act with the latter person is not worthless. [Or] if he performed an act of coition with this one first and then returned and performed an act of coition with the second, or performed an act of coition first and then made a statement [of intention of levirate marriage] with the second , or gave a Jewish bill of divorce to the second, or performed Halitzah with the second, there is nothing after an act of coition at all, and the final act is not considered, and he is permitted with the second’s [female] relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:5:2)


**אֵין אַחַר חֲלִיצָה כְלוּם.** אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי. וְאַסֵּיפָא צָרִיךְ לְמֵימַר אֵין אַחַר בִּיאָה כְּלוּם:


אין אחר חליצה כלום – It is referring to the first clause [of the Mishnah], but regarding the concluding clause, it is necessary to state that there is nothing after an act of coition.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:1)


**חָלַץ וְעָשָׂה מַאֲמָר נָתַן גֵּט וּבָעַל.** אַיָּבָם אֶחָד וִיבָמָה אַחַת קָאֵי. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּתָנָא לְעֵיל, הֲדַר תַּנְיָא הָכָא מִשּׁוּם סֵיפָא דְּבָעֵי לְאִפְלוּגֵי בֵּין בִּיאָה לַחֲלִיצָה, דַּחֲלִיצָה בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה בֵּין בָּאֶמְצַע בֵּין בַּסּוֹף אֵין אַחֲרֶיהָ כְּלוּם, וְהַבִּיאָה דַּוְקָא בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא בַּתְּחִלָּה. וּלְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן נַמִּי פְּלֻגְתָּא דְּרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה וְרַבָּנָן:


חלץ ועשה מאמר נתן גט ובעל – it refers to one levir and one widow whose husband died childless (i.e., Yevamah). And even though that this is taught above, [the Mishnah] teaches it again because of the concluding segment that is necessary to separate between an act of coition and Halitzah, for Halitzah, whether at the beginning, whether in the middle or whether at the end – there is nothing after it, and the act of intercourse especially at the time when it is at the beginning, and to teach us also the dispute of Rabbi Nehemiah and the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:2)


**בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה בֵּין בָּאֶמְצַע.** כְּגוֹן גֵּט לְזוֹ וְחָלַץ לְזוֹ וְחָזַר וְעָשָׂה בָּהּ מַאֲמָר אוֹ בְּבַעֲלַת הַגֵּט, לֹא מַהֲנֵי הַמַּאֲמָר וְלֹא בָּעִינַן גֵּט לְמַאֲמָרוֹ:


בין בתחלה בין באמצע – as, for example, [the delivery of] a Jewish bill of divorce to this one and performing Halitzah with that one, and then he went back and made a declaration of intention [to perform a levirate marriage] or with the woman who had the Jewish bill of divorce. The declaration of intention has no effect and we don’t require a Jewish bill of divorce to his declaration of intention [of desiring a levirate marriage].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:3)


**בַּסּוֹף.** אַחַר מַאֲמָר וְגֵט חָלַץ:


בסוף – After a declaration of intention [to perform a levirate marriage] and a Jewish bill of divorce, he performed Halitzah, there is nothing after it, for it is a complete Halitzah but if he retracted made a declaration of intention [of levirate marriage] , it doesn’t require a Jewish bill of divorce because Halitzah is a complete Jewish divorce, and even though it is was a disqualified Halitzah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:4)


**אֵין אַחֲרֶיהָ כְלוּם.** וַחֲלִיצָה גְּמוּרָה הִיא, וְאִי הֲדַר וְעָבֵיד מַאֲמָר לֹא בַּעְיָא גֵּט, מִשּׁוּם דַּחֲלִיצָה גֵּרוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים נִינְהוּ, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהִיא חֲלִיצָה פְּסוּלָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:5)


**הַבִּיאָה בָּאֶמְצַע.** כְּגוֹן גֵּט לְזוֹ וּבָעַל לְזוֹ וְחָזַר וְעָשָׂה מַאֲמָר בַּשְּׁלִישִׁית, אָסוּר בִּקְרוֹבוֹתֶיהָ:


הביאה באמצע – as in the case of a Jewish bill of divorce to this one and he performed an act of coition to the other woman and then he returned and made a declaration of intention with the third, he prohibited [to marry] her relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:6)


**בַּסּוֹף.** גֵּט לְזוֹ וּמַאֲמָר לְזוֹ וְחָזַר וּבָא (עליה) עַל [הָאַחַת], יֵשׁ אַחַר בִּיאָה זוֹ זִקַּת יְבָמִין, וְכִי מַפִּיק לָהּ בָּעֵי חֲלִיצָה וְלֹא סַגִּי לָהּ בְּגֵט:


בסוף – a Jewish bill of divorce to this one and a declaration of intention [to perform a levirate marriage] to that one, and then he retracted and came [upon her] on [the first, there is after this act of coition the levirate connection and when he releases her, he requires Halitzah and [the reception of] a Jewish bill of divorce is not enough for her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/5:6:7)


**אֵין אַחֲרֶיהָ כְלוּם.** וְנָפְקָא בְּגֵט בְּלֹא חֲלִיצָה. וְאִי הֲדַר וְקִדֵּשׁ לְאִידָךְ אַחַר בִּיאָה אֵין בְּמַעֲשָׂיו כְּלוּם:


אין אחריה כלום – and she leaves with a Jewish bill of divorce, without Halitzah, for if he retracted and betrothed to the other one after the act of coition, his deeds are not worth anything.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:1)


**הַבָּא עַל יְבִמְתּוֹ.** שׁוֹגֵג. כְּסָבוּר אִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת הִיא:


הבא על יבמתו. שוגג – as he was thinking that she was another woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:2)


**מֵזִיד.** לְשֵׁם זְנוּת וְלֹא לְמִצְוַת יִבּוּם:


מזיד – for the sake of unchastity and not for the Mitzvah of levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:3)


**אֲפִלּוּ הוּא שׁוֹגֵג וְהִיא מְזִידָה.** לֹא מִבַּעְיָא הֵיכָא דְּהִיא מִכַּוְּנָה לְמִצְוָה, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ הֵיכָא דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ לֹא מְכַוְּנֵי לְמִצְוָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהוּא מְכַוֵּן לְאִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת וְהִיא נִתְכַּוְּנָה לְשֵׁם זְנוּת, קָנָה, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (דברים כה) יְבָמָהּ יָבֹא עָלֶיהָ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם:


אפילו הוא שוגג והיא מזידה – needless to say where she intended [to fulfill] the Mitzvah but rather even where both of them don’t have the intention [of fulfilling] the Mitzvah such as the case where he intends for another woman, and she had the intention for the sake of unchastity, he has acquired her [as his wife], as it states in Scripture (Deuteronomy 25:5): “ Her husband’s brother shall unite with her: [he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty],” anyway.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:4)


**הַמְּעָרֶה.** שֶׁלֹּא גָּמַר בִּיאָתוֹ אֶלָּא הִכְנִיס עֲטָרָה בִּלְבַד. וּפְגִיעַת עֲטָרָה בְּלֹא הַכְנָסָה קְרוּיָה נְשִׁיקָה:


המערה – that he did not complete his coition, but rather, he inserted the corona of his membrum virile alone, and the meeting of the corona without insertion is called “kissing.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:5)


**קָנָה.** וְזָכָה בַּנַּחֲלָה, וְיוֹצֵאת בְּגֵט אִם בָּא לְהוֹצִיאָהּ:


קנה – and he took possession of the inheritance and she is removed with a Jewish bill of divorce if he comes to remove her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:1:6)


**לֹא חָלַק בֵּין בִּיאָה לְבִיאָה.** בֵּין בִּיאָה כְּדַרְכָּהּ לְבִיאָה שֶׁלֹּא כְּדַרְכָּהּ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כ) מִשְׁכְּבֵי אִשָּׁה, מַגִּיד הַכָּתוּב שֶׁשְּׁתֵּי מִשְׁכָּבוֹת יֵשׁ בְּאִשָּׁה:


לא חלק בין ביאה לביאה – whether a coition in its proper manner or a coition not in its proper manner, as it is written (Leviticus 20:13): “[If a man lies with a male] as one lies with a woman,” the Biblical verse tells us that there are two ways of sexual gratification.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:1)


**וְכֵן הַבָּא עַל אַחַת מִכָּל הָעֲרָיוֹת.** בְּאַחַת מִכָּל הַבִּיאוֹת הָאֵלּוּ:


וכן הבא על אחת מכל העריות – in one of all of these forms of coition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:2)


**אוֹ עַל הַפְּסוּלוֹת כְּגוֹן אַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל וְכוּ'.** פְּסָלָהּ לִכְהֻנָּה מִשּׁוּם זוֹנָה. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵשֶׁת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה מֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעֲלָהּ, פְּסוּלָה הִיא לִכְהֻנָּה. וּגְרוּשָׁה נַמִּי, אִי בַּת כֹּהֵן הִיא מִפְסְלָה בְּהַךְ בִּיאָה מִתְּרוּמָה דְּבֵי נַשָּׁא:


או על הפסולות כגון אלמנה לכהן גדול וכו' – she is invalidated to the priesthood because she is unfit to marry a Kohen (as a harlot), even though that the wife of an Israelite who was raped is permitted to her husband, but she is invalid to the priesthood, and a divorcee as well, if she is the daughter of a Kohen, she invalidates herself by this coition from Terumah of wife’s house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:2:3)


**מַמְזֶרֶת וּנְתִינָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** לָאו אַפְּסוּלָה לִכְהֻנָּה קָאֵי, דְּהָא פְּסוּלָה וְקַיְמָא, אֶלָּא לְעִנְיַן הַעֲרָאָה נָקַט לֵיהּ, לְמִלְקֵי עֲלָהּ בְּהַעֲרָאָה כִּגְמַר בִּיאָה:


ממזרת ונתינה לישראל – it dos not refer to her being invalidated to the priesthood, for she is invalidated and enduring, but rather to the matter of the first stage of sexual connection, the Mishnah took it as punishing her as the first stage of sexual connection which is like the completion of coition.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:1)


**מִן הָאֵרוּסִין לֹא יֹאכְלוּ בַתְּרוּמָה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשָׁה קִדּוּשֵׁי עֲבֵרָה נִפְסְלָה מִתְּרוּמָה דְּבֵית אָבִיהָ אִם בַּת כֹּהֵן הִיא:


מן האירוסין לא יאכלו בתרומה – because she was betrothed in a sinful betrothal, she is disqualified from the Terumah/priest’s due of her father’s house if she is the daughter of a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַכְשִׁירִין.** עַד שֶׁתִּבָּעֵל וְתֵעָשֶׂה חֲלָלָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ויקרא כא) וְלֹא יְחַלֵּל, שְׁנֵי חִלּוּלִין, אֶחָד לָהּ וְאֶחָד לְזַרְעָהּ. וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתַיְהוּ, אֶלָּא אַף מִן הָאֵרוּסִין לֹא יֹאכְלוּ בַתְּרוּמָה:


רבי אליעזר ור"ש מכשירין – until she has sexual intercourse and becomes the female issue of a priest’s illegitimate connection (or a priest’s wife illegitimately married to him), as it states (Leviticus 21:15): “that he may not profane [his offspring among his kin, for I the LORD have sanctified him],” two profanations: one- hers, and the other to her seed. But the Halakha is not according to him, but rather, even from betrothal, they should not consume Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:3)


**נִתְאַרְמְלוּ אוֹ נִתְגָּרְשׁוּ.** מִן הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלָּלוּ:


נתארמלו או נתגרשו – [became widowed or divorced] from these Kohanim from the marital state, they are disqualified, for they are considered an illegitimate connection to a Kohen with their sexual actions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:4)


**מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִין פְּסוּלוֹת.** דְּשַׁוְּיוּהָ חֲלָלָה בְּבִיאָתָן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:3:5)


**מִן הָאֵרוּסִין כְּשֵׁרוֹת.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא לֹא פָּסַל מִן הָאֵרוּסִין אֶלָּא בְּחַיֵּיהֶן, שֶׁהִיא מִשְׁתַּמֶּרֶת לְבִיאָה פְּסוּלָה, אֲבָל מֵתוּ לֹא:


מן הארוסין כשרות – and even the first Tanna/teacher [of our Mishnah] did not invalidate from the betrothal, other than while they are alive for she has to be on her guard to an invalid coition, but not if they (i.e., the men) died.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:4:1)


**לֹא יִשָּׂא אֶת הַבּוֹגֶרֶת.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְהוּא אִשָּׁה בִּבְתוּלֶיהָ יִקָּח, פְּרָט לְבוֹגֶרֶת שֶׁכָּלוּ בְּתוּלֶיהָ:


לא ישא את הבוגרת – as it is written (Leviticus 21:13): “He (i.e., the High Priest) may only marry a woman who is a virgin,” excluding a woman of age, whose virginity was destroyed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:4:2)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַכְשִׁירִין.** וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתַיְהוּ:


רבי אליעזר ורבי שמעון מכשירין – and the Halakha is not according to their opinions.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:5:1)


**כֹּהֵן הֶדְיוֹט לֹא יִשָּׂא אַיְלוֹנִית וְכוּ'.** וְהוּא הַדִּין לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְלֹא נָקַט כֹּהֵן אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם פְּלֻגְתָּא דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר דְּכֹהֵן אֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ לוֹ אִשָּׁה וּבָנִים לֹא יִשָּׂא אַיְלוֹנִית, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


כהן הדיוט לא ישא אילונית וכו' – and the same law applies for a Israelite male, and it (i.e., the Mishnah) did not take [the example of] a Kohen other than because of the dispute of Rabbi Yehuda who stated, that a Kohen, even if he has a wife and children, should not marry a barren woman, which is not the case regarding an Israelite male, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:5:2)


**וְשֶׁנִּבְעֲלָה בְּעִילַת זְנוּת.** כְּגוֹן חַיָּבֵי לָאוִין אוֹ חַיָּבֵי עֲשֵׂה, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן חַיָּבֵי כְּרִיתוּת וּמִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין. אֲבָל הַבָּא עַל הַפְּנוּיָה לֹא עֲשָׂאָהּ זוֹנָה:


ושנבעלה בעילת זנות – such as for example those liable for violation of negative commandments or those liable for violation of positive commandments, and all the more so, those liable for extirpation or death by the Jewish court , but a person who comes upon a unmarried woman has not made her a harlot.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:1)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים.** דְּיָלְפִינַן מִמֹּשֶׁה שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ מִן הָאִשָּׁה לְאַחַר שֶׁנּוֹלְדוּ לוֹ שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים:


ב"ש אומרים שני זכרים – that we derive it from Moses who separated from [his] wife afer two males were born to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:2)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה בְּרָאָם.** וּמִמֹּשֶׁה אֵין רְאָיָה, שֶׁעַל פִּי הַדִּבּוּר עָשָׂה:


וב"ה אומרים זכר ונקיבה – as it states (Genesis 5:2): “male and female He created them.” But from Moses, it is not a proof because he acted following the Divine order.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:3)


**וְשָׁהָה עִמָּהּ עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים וְכוּ'.** כִּדְאַשְׁכְּחָן בְּאַבְרָהָם, לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁהָה עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים עִם שָׂרָה וְלֹא יָלְדָה נָשָׂא הָגָר. וְיָמִים שֶׁיָּשַׁב עִמָּהּ בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ לֹא עָלוּ מִן הַמִּנְיָן, דְּדִלְמָא יְשִׁיבַת חוּץ לָאָרֶץ גָּרְמָה. וְכָל הַיָּמִים שֶׁהָאִישׁ אוֹ הָאִשָּׁה חוֹלִים אוֹ חֲבוּשִׁים בְּבֵית הָאֲסוּרִים וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּזֶה מִדְּבָרִים שֶׁמּוֹנְעִים הַתַּשְׁמִישׁ, אֵין עוֹלִין לְמִנְיַן עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים:


ושהה עמה עשר שנים וכו' – as we find with Abraham after he tarried with his wife for ten years without her giving birth, he married Hagar and for the days that he lived with her outside the Land [of Israel], the did not count in the number, for perhaps dwelling outside of the land caused it, and all the days that the husband or the wife are sick or are imprisoned and in a similar manner we speak that they prevent sexual intimacy, they do not count to the number of ten years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:4)


**גֵּרְשָׁהּ מֻתֶּרֶת לִנָּשֵׂא לְאַחֵר.** דְּשֶׁמָּא לָאו מִנָּהּ הֲוֵי אֶלָּא מִנֵּיהּ. וּכְשֶׁהוּא מְגָרְשָׁהּ נוֹתֵן לָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ אִם טוֹעֶנֶת דְּמִנֵּיהּ הוּא שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹרֶה כְּחֵץ, וּמַטִּילִין חֵרֶם עָלֶיהָ אִם יוֹדַעַת שֶׁהוּא יוֹרֶה כְּחֵץ וְטוֹעֶנֶת עָלָיו טַעֲנַת שֶׁקֶר:


גרשה מותרת לינשא לאחר – for perhaps it was not her, but rather him [who was infertile], and when he divorces her, he gives her the Ketubah settlement if she claims that it is from him [who was infertile] who does not have a spermatic emission which does not shoot forth like an arrow (i.e., does not fructify), and they place an oath upon her if she knows that he does have a spermatic emission which does shoot forth like an arrow , and she makes against him false claim which is a lie.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:5)


**הָאִישׁ מְצֻוֶּה עַל פְּרִיָּה וּרְבִיָּה וְלֹא הָאִשָּׁה.** דִּכְתִיב (בראשית א) פְּרוּ וּרְבוּ וְגוֹ'‏ וְכִבְשׁוּהָ, וְכִבְשָׁהּ כְּתִיב חָסֵר וָי"ו, הָאִישׁ שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לִכְבֹּשׁ אֶת הָאִשָּׁה הוּא מְצֻוֶּה עַל פְּרִיָּה וּרְבִיָּה:


האיש מצווה על פריה ורביה ולא האשה – as it is written (Genesis 1:28): “Be fertile and increase, [fill the earth] and master it,” and [the word] "כבשה" is written without a “VAV.” It is the way for a man to conquer the woman; he is commanded on “being fertile and increase.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 6:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/6:6:6)


**רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָא אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


רבי יוחנן בן ברוקה אומר וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to him.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:1:1)


**אַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** עַבְדֵי מְלוֹג. מַה שֶּׁהָאִשָּׁה מְשַׁיֶּרֶת לְעַצְמָהּ וְאֵינָהּ כּוֹתֶבֶת בִּכְתֻבָּתָהּ, נִקְרָאִים נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג, שֶׁהַבַּעַל מוֹלֵג אוֹתָם נְכָסִים כִּמְלִיגַת הַתַּרְנְגוֹלִים, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת אוֹתָן נְכָסִים, וְאִם פָּחֲתוּ פָּחֲתוּ לָהּ וְאִם הוֹתִירוּ הוֹתִירוּ לָהּ. וְנִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל הֵם הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁהִכְנִיסָה לוֹ וּמַה שֶּׁהוֹסִיף הוּא לָהּ וְכָתְבוּ בַּכְּתֻבָּה סַךְ הַכֹּל קִבֵּל עָלָיו פְּלוֹנִי כָּךְ וְכָךְ בִּכְתֻבָּתָהּ. וּלְכָךְ נִקְרָאִים צֹאן בַּרְזֶל, שֶׁהַקֶּרֶן קַיָּם כְּבַרְזֶל, שֶׁאִם מֵתוּ כֻּלָּן, חַיָּב הַבַּעַל לְשַׁלֵּם. וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ רְגִילִים לָשׁוּם הַצֹּאן בְּשׁוּמַת כָּךְ וְכָךְ, וְהָיָה הָרוֹעֶה חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן כְּפִי הַשּׁוּמָא אֲפִלּוּ אִם מֵתוּ כָּל הַצֹּאן, לְכָךְ נִקְרְאוּ הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁהַבַּעַל מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו אַחֲרָיוּתָן נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל:


אלמנה לכ"ג: עבדי מלוג – what the woman leaves for herself and does not write in her Ketubah is called


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:1:2)


**עַבְדֵי מְלוֹג לֹא יֹאכְלוּ.** לְפִי שֶׁהֵן שֶׁלָּהּ וְהִיא חֲלָלָה:


נכסי (עבדי) מלוג – a wife’s estate of which the husband has the fruition without responsibility for loss or deterioration - that the husband “plucks” that property like the plucking of chickens because he consumes the fruits of those properties and if they lessened [In value] they lessened for her, and if they increased, they increased for her. But נכסי צאן ברזל (mort-main, the wife’s estate held by her husband, which, in the case of her death or divorce, he must restore “in specie,” being responsible with all his landed property for loss or deterioration) – they are the properties that she brought in to him and what he adds to it is hers, and they wrote into the Ketubah: the total amount that so-and-so accepted upon himself is such -and such, in her Ketubah, and therefore, they are called צאן ברזל – as the principal exists like iron, and if everyone died, the husband is liable to because they are accustomed to place the sheep in such-and-such an appraisement of valuables (brought into the marriage by the wife) , and the shepherd would be responsible for them (if lost), according to the estimate even if all the sheep died, hence, they are called properties that the husband accepts responsibility for - נכסי צאן ברזל (that must be returned to the wife “in specie” in the event of death or divorce.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:2:1)


**בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְכֹהֵן.** אֲפִלּוּ עַבְדֵי מְלוֹג יֹאכְלוּ, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוֵי קִנְיָן כֹּהֵן שֶׁקָּנָה קִנְיָן, כְּלוֹמַר הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהִיא קִנְיָנוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן קָנְתָה אֵלּוּ הָעֲבָדִים, וּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וְכֹהֵן כִּי יִקְנֶה נֶפֶשׁ קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ, שֶׁאֵין תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ, אֶלָּא לְמִדְרָשׁ הָכִי, וְכֹהֵן כִּי יִקְנֶה קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ נֶפֶשׁ, שֶׁעַבְדּוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן קָנָה נֶפֶשׁ, הוּא יֹאכַל בּוֹ:


בת ישראל שנשאת לכהן – even מגול slaves (i.e., that the husband has the fruition without responsibility for loss or deterioration) , they may consume [Terumah/priest’s due] because they are the acquisition of a Kohen who purchased merchandise, meaning to say, the wife that is his purchase of a Kohen who purchased these particular slaves, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): “But a person who is a priest’s property by purchase [may eat of them (i.e., the sacred donations) and those born into his household may eat of his food],” for the inference of "קנין כספו"/”the priest’s property” does not come other than for this exegesis: that a Kohen who has as purchased property with his money, that soul, which is the slave of the Kohen who bought a soul may eat of it (i.e., his food as a member of his household).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:1)


**וְהִנִּיחָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ בָּנִים מִמֶּנּוּ וְהִיא אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה, לֹא יֹאכְלוּ עַבְדֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל בַּתְּרוּמָה, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל יוֹרְשִׁין וְיֵשׁ לָעֻבָּר חֵלֶק בָּהֶן, וְאֵין לָעֻבָּר כֹּחַ לְהַאֲכִילָם בַּתְּרוּמָה, אִי מִשּׁוּם דְּקָסָבַר עֻבָּר בִּמְעֵי זָרָה זָר הוּא, אִי מִשּׁוּם דְּקָסָבַר יָלוּד מַאֲכִיל, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָלוּד אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וִילִיד בֵּיתוֹ הֵם יֹאכְלוּ, וְקָרֵי בֵּיהּ הֵם יַאֲכִילוּ:


והניחה מעוברת – even though she has children from him and she consumes Terumah, the slaves of her mort-main do not eat Terumah because they belong to the heirs, and the fetus has a portion in them and the fetus does not have the ability to provide them with Terumah either because he holds that a fetus in a foreign womb (of a non-Kohen), he is a foreigner or because he holds that someone born can feed; someone who is not born cannot feed, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): “and those that are born into his household may eat [of his food],” and we call him they who will feed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:2)


**שֶׁהָעֻבָּר פּוֹסֵל.** אִם בַּת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא וְהִנִּיחָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת וְאֵין לָהּ בֵּן אַחֵר, פּוֹסְלָהּ מִלָּשׁוּב לְבֵית אָבִיהָ:


שהעובר פוסל – if the daughter of a Kohen is married to an Israelite [male], and she was left pregnant (because her husband died), and she has no other son, it disqualifies her from returning to her father’s home.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:3)


**וְאֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל.** שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֵן וְהִנִּיחָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת אֵין בָּעֻבָּר כֹּחַ לְהַאֲכִילָהּ, וְהוּא הַדִּין לַעֲבָדָיו:


ואינו מאכיל – if she was the daughter of an Israelite married to a Kohen and she was left pregnant (because her husband died), the fetus does not have the strength to sustain her and the same law applies to his slaves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:3:4)


**אַף בַּת כֹּהֵן לְכֹהֵן כוּ'.** כֵּיוָן דְּאָמַרְתְּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָלוּד אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל, אַף בַּת כֹּהֵן לְכֹהֵן אֵין הָעֲבָדִים אוֹכְלִים בַּתְּרוּמָה מִפְּנֵי חֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל עֻבָּר, שֶׁהֲרֵי עֲבָדָיו הֵן וְאֵינָן אוֹכְלִין אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִילוֹ, וְהוּא אֵין בּוֹ כֹּחַ לְהַאֲכִיל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אף בת כהן לכהן כו' – since you said that he is not born, he does not sustain, even the daughter of a Kohen who is married to a Kohen, the slaves don’t consume Terumah because of the fetus’ portion because they are his slaves and they don’t consume, other than for him, and he lacks the strength to sustain them, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:1)


**הָעֻבָּר.** הַאי עֻבָּר, אִי בַּת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, פּוֹסֵל, דִּכְתִיב בִּנְעוּרֶיהָ, פְּרָט לִמְעֻבֶּרֶת. אִי בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֵן הִיא, לֹא מַאֲכִיל, דְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ יָלוּד אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל:


העובר והיבם וכו' -this fetus if [it is the product of] the daughter of a Kohen and an Israelite [male], it disqualifies her [from eating Terumah] as it is written (Leviticus 22:13): “[But if the priest’s daughter is widowed or divorced and without offspring, and is back in her father’s house] as in her youth, [she may eat of her father’s food],” excluding someone pregnant, if she is an Israelite woman [awaiting the act of a levir] to a Kohen, he does not feed [Terumah] and that which is not born does not cause feed [either].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:2)


**וְהַיָּבָם.** אִי בַּת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, פּוֹסֵל לָהּ, וְשָׁבָה אֶל בֵּית אָבִיהָ כְּתִיב (שם), פְּרָט לְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם שֶׁאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לָשׁוּב, שֶׁהִיא אֲגוּדָה בִּיבָמָהּ. וְאִי בַת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֵן, לֹא מַאֲכִיל לָהּ, קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא וְזוֹ קִנְיַן אָחִיו הִיא:


והיבם – if the daughter of a Kohen is [to engage in a levirate marriage] with an Israelite man, he disqualifies her [from partaking of Terumah] as it is written (Leviticus 22:13): “and is back in her father’s house,” excluding the widow waiting for her brother-in-law to act, who is not able to return [to her father’s house] because she is in union with the widow waiting for her brother-in-law to act and if it is the daughter of an Israelite [who is to engage in a levirate marriage] with a Kohen, he does not feed her [Terumah]. The All-Merciful said (Leviticus 22:11): “[But a person who is a priest’s] property by purchase [may eat of them],” but this is the purchase of his brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/7:4:3)


**וְהָאֵרוּסִין.** אִי בַת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, פָּסִיל לָהּ, דְּקָנֵי לָהּ בַּהֲוָיָה, וּמִשְּׁעַת הֲוָיָה אִפְסִילָה לָהּ, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וּבַת כֹּהֵן כִּי תִּהְיֶה לְאִישׁ זָר. אִי בַת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֵן, לֹא מַאֲכִיל לָהּ, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְזְגוּ לָהּ כּוֹס יַיִן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ וְתַשְׁקֶה לְאַחֶיהָ וּלְאַחְיוֹתֶיהָ:
