**חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת.** שֶׁמָּא נִתַּן לָהּ יָבָם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ דְּאָמַר אֵינָהּ חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת:


חוששת – perhaps she had been given a levir. But the Halakha not according to Rabbi Yehoshua who said that she does not have to worry/scruple [concerning her].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:1)


**שְׁתֵּי יְבָמוֹת.** נְשֵׁי שְׁנֵי אַחִים:


שתי יבמות – the wives of two brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:2)


**מִפְּנֵי בַעְלָהּ שֶׁל זוֹ.** שֶׁמָּא הוּא חַי וּזְקוּקָה לוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאִשְׁתּוֹ אוֹמֶרֶת מֵת, אֵינָהּ נֶאֱמֶנֶת לְהַתִּיר זוֹ לַשּׁוּק עַל פִּיהָ, שֶׁאֵין יְבָמָה מְעִידָה לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


מפני בעלה של זו – perhaps he is living and she is bound/chained to him, and even though that his wife states that he has died, she is not believed to permit this one (i.e., the woman) to the whole world through her [words on her] lips for the widow of a brother who died without issue does not provide testimony on her fellow widow of a brother who died without issue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:3)


**לָזוֹ עֵדִים.** שֶׁמֵּת בַּעֲלָהּ, וְלָזוֹ אֵין לָהּ עֵדִים:


לזו עדים – whose husband died and the other [woman whose husband died] does not have witnesses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:4)


**זוֹ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לָהּ עֵדִים אֲסוּרָה.** לִנָּשֵׂא לַשּׁוּק, מִפְּנֵי יְבָמָהּ, שֶׁאֵין עֵדִים שֶׁמֵּת:


זו שיש לה עדים אסורה – to marry to the entire world, for she is a widow of a brother who died without issue who did not have witnesses that he died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:5)


**וְזוֹ שֶׁאֵין לָהּ עֵדִים מֻתֶּרֶת.** שֶׁהֲרֵי בִּשְׁבִיל בַּעֲלָהּ אֵינָהּ נֶאֱסֶרֶת, שֶׁהִיא נֶאֱמֶנֶת לוֹמַר מֵת בַּעֲלִי, וּבִשְׁבִיל יְבָמָהּ נַמִּי לֹא מִתְסְרָא, שֶׁהֲרֵי בָּאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁמֵּת:


וזו שאין לה עדים מותרת – for on account of her husband, she is not forbidden, for she is believed to state: “my husband died,” and because of her being a widow of a brother who died without issue also, she is not forbidden for witnesses came testifying that he had died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:6)


**לָזוֹ בָּנִים וְלָזוֹ אֵין בָּנִים.** וְאֵין עֵדִים לֹא לָזוֹ וְלֹא לָזוֹ, אֶת שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ בָּנִים מֻתֶּרֶת, וְאֶת שֶׁאֵין לָהּ בָּנִים אֲסוּרָה:


לזו בנים ולזו אין בנים – and there are witnesses neither to this one nor to that one; the woman who has children is permitted [to remarry] and the one who has no children is prohibited [from remarrying].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:7)


**נִתְיַבְּמוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ לָהֶן כָּאן שְׁנֵי יְבָמִין וְנִתְיַבְּמוּ וּמֵתוּ הַיְּבָמִים, אֲסוּרוֹת לְהִנָּשֵׂא לַשּׁוּק, זוֹ מִפְּנֵי בַּעֲלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל זוֹ וְזוֹ מִפְּנֵי בַּעֲלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל זוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן נְשׂוּאוֹת לִיבָמֵיהֵן בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁמֵּתוּ בַּעֲלֵיהֶן, עַל פִּי עַצְמָן נִשְּׂאוּ, וְהָיוּ נֶאֱמָנוֹת, שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה שֶׁאָמְרָה מֵת בַּעֲלִי תִּתְיַבֵּם. אֲבָל עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁתִּהְיֶינָה נְשׂוּאוֹת לַשּׁוּק לֹא, שֶׁאִם יִנָּשְׂאוּ לַשּׁוּק נִמְצָא עֵדוּתָהּ שֶׁל זוֹ מוֹעִיל לָזוֹ וְעֵדוּתָהּ שֶׁל זוֹ מוֹעִיל לָזוֹ, וְאֵין הַיְּבָמוֹת מְעִידוֹת זוֹ לָזוֹ:


נתיבמו – that they had here two widows of brothers who died without issue and they married their levirs and the levirs died, they are forbidden to marry anyone in the whole world: this one (i.e., the first woman), because of the first husband of the other one, and that one (i.e., the second woman) because of the first husband of the other one, even though both were married to their levirs because of the presumption that their husbands had died, through their own testimony hey married, and they were believed, for the woman who said: “my husband died,” should be married by her levir. But now, that they would will be married to anyone in the world, no, for if they would marry to anyone in the world, it would be found that the testimony of this woman would be beneficial to that one (i.e., woman) and the testimony of that one (i.e., woman) would be beneficial to the other (i.e. woman), and widows of brothers who died without issue cannot give testimony one for the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:2:8)


**הוֹאִיל וְהֻתְּרוּ לַיְבָמִין.** בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁמֵּתוּ בַּעֲלֵיהֶן, הֻתְּרוּ לִנָּשֵׂא לַשּׁוּק, דְּתוּ לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הֵם בַּחַיִּים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר:


הואיל והותרו ליבמין – on the presumption their husbands had died, they were permitted to marry anyone in the world, and furthermore, we don’t suspect lest that they are living, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eleazar.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:1)


**פַּרְצוּף פָּנִים עִם הַחֹטֶם.** אִם לֹא רָאָהוּ בְּפַרְצוּף פָּנָיו, אוֹ שֶׁנִּטַּל חָטְמוֹ, אֵין מְעִידִין לְהַשִּׂיא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ, שֶׁמָּא אֵינוֹ הוּא:


פרצוף פנים עם החוטם – if he did not see him with the appearance of his face or that his nose was taken , we don’t testify to permit [someone else] to marry his wife, for perhaps it is not him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:2)


**מְגֻיָּד.** מְנֻתָּח. כְּמוֹ גֹּדוּ אִלָּנָא (דניאל ד):


מגוייד – dissected like [Daniel 4:14]: “Hew down the tree, [lop off is branches].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:3)


**אוֹ חַיָּה אוֹכֶלֶת בּוֹ.** דַּוְקָא בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין נַפְשׁוֹ יוֹצְאָה, אֲבָל בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁנַּפְשׁוֹ יוֹצְאָה, מֵעִיד עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא מֵת:


או חיה אוכלת בו – specifically in he place where his soul does not depart from [his body], but if it in the place where the soul departs, he testifies about him that he has died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:4)


**אֵין מְעִידִין אֶלָּא עַד שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים.** אִם לֹא רָאוּהוּ עַד לְאַחַר שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים לְמִיתָתוֹ אֵין מְעִידִין עָלָיו, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאִית פָּנָיו וְאֵינוֹ זֶה שֶׁהֵם סְבוּרִים:


אין מעידין אלא עד ג' ימים – if they did not see him until after three days of his death, we don’t testify regarding him, for we suspect lest the appearance of his face has changed and this [individual] is not that whom they think [that it is].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:3:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶּן בָּבָא אוֹמֵר לֹא כָל אָדָם וְכוּ'.** יֵשׁ לְךָ אָדָם שֶׁמְּמַהֵר לְהִנָּפֵּחַ, כְּגוֹן אָדָם שָׁמֵן. וְיֵשׁ מָקוֹם שֶׁאָדָם מְמַהֵר לְהַסְרִיחַ וּלְהִשְׁתַּנּוֹת בּוֹ כְּגוֹן מְקוֹם חַמָּה. וְיֵשׁ לְךָ שָׁעָה שֶׁהָעוֹלָם חַם וְאָדָם מְמַהֵר לְהַסְרִיחַ וּלְהִשְׁתַּנּוֹת. וְהַכֹּל לְפִי הָאָדָם וְהַמָּקוֹם וְהַשָּׁעָה, בֵּין לְקֻלָּא בֵּין לְחֻמְרָא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה בן בבא אומר לא כל אדם וכו' – there are people who speedily blows/swells up, such as a heavy persn, and there is a place where a person speedily decomposes and to change, such as in a hot place, and there is a time when the world is hot and a person speedily decomposes and changes, and everything is according to the individual, and the place and the time, whether for leniency or for stringency and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:1)


**מַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם סוֹף.** שֶׁנִּרְאִים כָּל אַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת שֶׁסְּבִיב הַמַּיִם. וּמַיִם שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם סוֹף, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לִרְאוֹת כָּל סְבִיבָיו. וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר לֹא מַפְלִיג בֵּין מַיִם שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם סוֹף לְמַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם סוֹף. אֲבָל חֲכָמִים פְּלִיגֵי בִּבְרַיְתָא, וְאָמְרֵי, מַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם סוֹף אִשְׁתּוֹ מֻתֶּרֶת אִם שָׁהָה שָׁם שִׁעוּר שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיִּחְיֶה כְּשִׁעוּר זֶה בְּתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם, וּמַיִם שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם סוֹף אִשְׁתּוֹ אֲסוּרָה, דְּשֶׁמָּא לְאַחַר שֶׁעָלָה מִשָּׁם יָצָא וְהָלַךְ לוֹ, כִּדְאָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּמַתְנִיתִין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מים שיש להם סוף – that appears all four directions that surround the water and water that is not in sight of shore, that a person cannot see all about him. But Rabbi Meir does not make a distinction between a body of water that is not within sight of shore and a body of water that is within sight of shore but the Sages dispute in the Baraita (see Tosefta Yevamot, Chapter 14, Halakha 5 and Talmud Yevamot 121a) and state that a body of water that is within sight of shore, his wife is permitted if he tarried there a period of time where it is impossible that he would live, according to this period of time within the water. But water that is not in sight of shore, his wife is forbidden [to remarry] for lest after he came up from there, he left and went on his way, as Rabbi Yosi stated in our Mishnah, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:2)


**וְעָלָה לְאַחַר שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר אָדָם חַי בְּתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם יָמִים רַבִּים, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי אֲפִלּוּ בְּמַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם סוֹף דְּאִי נָפֵיק הֲוָה חָזֵי לֵיהּ, חַיִישׁ דִּלְמָא נָפֵיק בָּתַר יָמִים רַבִּים וְלֹא חָזוּ לֵיהּ. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי פָּלֵיג עֲלֵיהּ וְאָמַר מַעֲשֶׂה בְּסוּמָא שֶׁיָּרַד לִטְבֹּל בַּמְּעָרָה וְכוּ', וּמְעָרָה מַיִם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם סוֹף, וְשָׁהוּ עַד שֶׁתֵּצֵא נַפְשָׁם וְהִשִּׂיאוּ אֶת נְשׁוֹתֵיהֶן:


ועלה לאחר שלשה ימים – for Rabbi Meir holds that a person can live within the water for a number of days, and because of this, even in water that is within sight of a shore, that if he comes out, someone will see him, for we suspect that perhaps after he would come out after many days and they wouldn’t see him. But Rabbi Yosi disputes on this and says that there is an incident about a blind person who went down to ritually immerse in a cave and the cave had water that is in sight of a shore and they remained until they died and their married off their wives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:4:3)


**מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַטָּה.** הוֹאִיל וְיָכוֹל לִחְיוֹת, לֹא תִנָּשֵׂא, שֶׁמָּא יָצָא וְלֹא רָאוּהוּ, דְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם סוֹף הֵן:


מן הארכובה ולמטה – since he would be able to live, [his wife] should not remarry lest he leaves and no one sees him, for they are a body of water that is not within sight of a shore.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:5:1)


**אֲפִלּוּ שָׁמַע מִן הַנָּשִׁים.** שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוְּנוֹת לְהָעִיד, יָכוֹל לֵילֵךְ לְהָעִיד לְהַשִּׂיא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


אפילו שמע מן השנשים – that they don’t intend to testify, he can go to testify so that his (i.e., the dead person) wife may marry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:5:2)


**הֲרֵי אָנוּ הוֹלְכִים לִסְפֹּד וְלִקְבֹּר אֶת אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ אָנוּ בָּאִים מִלִּסְפֹּד וְלִקְבֹּר אֶת אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי. וְצָרִיךְ נַמִּי שֶׁיִּשְׁמַע אוֹתָם מְסַפְּרִים מֵעִנְיְנֵי הַהֶסְפֵּד, כָּךְ וְכָךְ רַבָּנָן הֲווּ הָתָם, כָּךְ וְכָךְ סַפְדָנֵי הֲווּ הָתָם, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ תִּינוֹקוֹת לְשַׂחֵק וּלְהַעֲלוֹת שֵׁמוֹת, וְשֶׁמָּא נְמָלָה אוֹ חָגָב קָבְרוּ וּמְכַנִּים אוֹתוֹ בְּשֵׁם אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי:


הרי אנו הולכים לספוד ולקבור את איש פלוני – in the Gemara (Tractate Yevamot 121b) that one requires that they would say, “we come from eulogizing and burying this particular individual,” and it also requires that he would hear them telling of the matters of the eulogy: “such-and such Rabbis were there,” “such-and-such eulogies were there,” because it is the manner of young children to pay and to bring up names and lest it was an ant or locust that they buried, and bring them in with the name of that certain individual.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:6:1)


**וּמַשִּׂיאִים עַל פִּי בַת קוֹל.** [אִם] שָׁמְעוּ קוֹל צִוְחַת פְּלוֹנִי מֵת:


משיאין על פי בת קול – [if] they heard [the voice] crying out that so-and-so has died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:6:2)


**בְּצַלְמוֹן.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


בצלמון – the name of a place.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:1)


**וְנוּמֵתִי לוֹ.** אָמַרְתִּי לוֹ:


ונומיתי לו – I said to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:2)


**כֵּן הַדְּבָרִים.** שֶׁכָּל חֲבֵרָיו חוֹלְקִים עָלָיו:


כן הדברים – for all of his colleagues dispute him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:3)


**מְשֻׁבֶּשֶׁת בִּגְיָסוֹת.** וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לֵילֵךְ אֶצְלְכֶם לְהָעִיד מַה שֶּׁשָּׁמַעְתִּי, אֶלָּא אֱמֹר לָהֶם אַתָּה זֶה הָעֵדוּת בִּשְׁמִי:


משובשת בגייסות – and I am not able to go to you to testify on what I heart, but rather, say to them: You are who have offered testimony in my name.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמְרִים כוּ'‏ וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ. אֶלָּא הֲלָכָה מַשִּׂיאִים עַל פִּי אִשָּׁה וְעַל פִּי קְרוֹבִים חוּץ מֵחָמֵשׁ נָשִׁים דִּתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, וְעֵד מִפִּי עֵד, מִפִּי אִשָּׁה, מִפִּי עֶבֶד, מִפִּי שִׁפְחָה, כָּשֵׁר לְעֵדוּת אִשָּׁה:


ר' אליעזר ור' יהושע אומרים כו' ור"ע אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not like one of them bu rather the Halakha is that we marry on the word of a woman and on the word of relatives except for five women that are taught in our Mishnah (see Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 15, Mishnah 4). But on the evidence of a single witness, on the evidence of a woman, on the evidence of a slave, on the evidence of a maid servant is fit for the testimony of a woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:5)


**לֹא תְּהֵא כֹהֶנֶת כַּפֻּנְדָּקִית.** כְּלוֹמַר מְיֻחֶסֶת לֹא תְהֵא נֶאֱמֶנֶת כְּפֻנְדָּקִית גּוֹיָה, בִּתְמִיהַּ, וְאִם הֶאֱמִינוּ הַפּוּנְדָּקִית גּוֹיָה מֵסִיחָה לְפִי תֻּמָּהּ, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁיַּאֲמִינוּ בְּאִשָּׁה יִשְׂרְאֵלִית:


לא תהא כהנת כפונדקית – meaning to say, of distinguished birth, she is not believed like an heathen inn-hostess, in astonishment, and if they believed the heathen inn-hostess, she makes a statement in ignorance of its legal bearing, all the more so, that they would believe an Israelite woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Yevamot 16:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Yevamot/r/16:7:6)


**לִכְשֶׁתְּהֵא פֻּנְדָּקִית נֶאֱמֶנֶת.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁגַּם הַפֻּנְדָּקִית אֵינָהּ נֶאֱמֶנֶת:


לכתהא פונדקית נאמנת – meaning to say, that the inn-hostess is not believed.
